<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48968606" data-id="48968606" data-tags="Show HN|Hardware|Open Source" data-score="2881" data-comments="343" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" data-time="1784472076">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>2881</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>343</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48734373" data-id="48734373" data-tags="AI|Privacy" data-score="2395" data-comments="731" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373" data-time="1782834264">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=thereallo.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>thereallo.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</span></a><span class="dom">thereallo.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</span></p>

It emphasizes agent-driven workflows and scalable data access, signaling a push toward more transparent, configurable intelligence tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi 发表 K3，作为一个开放前沿情报平台，概述了其架构与在开放数据探索中的潜在应用场景。强调代理驱动的工作流与可扩展的数据访问，预示向更透明、可配置的情报工具迈进。</span></p>

It marks another step in upgrading instruction-following, safety controls, and performance for enterprise-grade AI assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Opus 5，Claude Opus 系列的最新版本，进一步提升了指令遵循、安全控制和企业级 AI 助手的性能。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://f-droid.org/2026/07/01/adv-malware.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android Developer Verification: Threat masquerading as protection</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证：伪装成保护的威胁</span></a><span class="dom">f-droid.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools.</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools. Such tactics exploit trust signals around security features to mislead users or bypass vetting, undermining platform defenses. The takeaway is a push for tighter vetting, clearer indicators of app behavior, and user skepticism when apps claim to protect you.</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。此类手段利用安全功能的信任信号误导用户或规避审核，削弱平台防护。要点在于加强审核、提供更清晰的应用行为指示，并提高用户在遇到自称保护工具的应用时的警惕。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/10/apple-sues-openai-trade-secret-theft/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple sues OpenAI, accuses ex-employees of stealing trade secrets</span><span class='zh'>苹果控告 OpenAI，指控前员工窃取商业机密</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff.</span><span class='zh'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff. The case centers on proprietary AI techniques and internal data siphoned during a transition, signaling growing tension as big tech fights over AI talent. The outcome could influence how firms protect confidential knowledge during acquisitions and departures.</span><span class='zh'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。此案凸显大型科技公司在人才流动与知识保护方面的博弈，将影响企业在并购和员工离职时的机密信息防护策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/eu-parliament-greenlights-chat-control-1-0-breyer-our-children-lose-out/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU Parliament greenlights Chat Control 1.0</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会通过聊天监控1.0，Breyer：我们的孩子要吃亏</span></a><span class="dom">patrick-breyer.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention.</span><span class='zh'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention. Critics warn that the measure expands surveillance at the expense of privacy and free expression, while Breyer argues that children will lose out.</span><span class='zh'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。批评者担心此举侵蚀隐私与言论自由，而Breyer则认为这会让儿童吃亏。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/hugging-face-model-evaluation-security-incident/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face address security incident during model evaluation</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 解决模型评估中的安全事件</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms. They outline the investigation steps, remediation actions, and ongoing safeguards to prevent data leaks and misuse during evaluation. The episode underscores the fragility of cross-platform evaluation pipelines and the need for hardened security controls in AI collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。此次事件凸显跨平台评估流程的脆弱性，以及在 AI 领域加强安全控制的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://raymyers.org/post/zed-creator-calls-spade-a-spade/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zig Creator Calls Spade a Spade, Anthropic Blows Smoke</span><span class='zh'>Zig 创作者直言不讳，Anthropic 敷衍其词</span></a><span class="dom">raymyers.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency. The controversy underscores the tension between ambitious safety promises and practical concerns from developers who build on AI tools. It highlights how independent voices can shape public scrutiny of AI vendors.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。此事凸显了安全承诺的宏大叙事与开发者对 AI 工具的实际关切之间的张力。独立声音可能影响对 AI 供应商的公众监督。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nealstephenson.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nealstephenson.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing by hand is good for your brain</span><span class='zh'>手写对大脑有益</span></a><span class="dom">nealstephenson.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks.</span><span class='zh'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks. The act of forming letters seems to strengthen neural connections that support long-term memory and understanding. For students and knowledge workers, mixing handwritten notes with digital tools can improve retention.</span><span class='zh'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。手写过程有助于强化支持长期记忆和理解的神经连接。对于学生和知识工作者，混合使用手写笔记与数字工具有利于提升记忆留存。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Health' style='--c:#4ade80'>Health</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1455</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>655</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48829312" data-id="48829312" data-tags="Security" data-score="1416" data-comments="226" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829312" data-time="1783500366">
<a class="thumb" href="https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='tris.sherliker.net' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decoding the obfuscated bash script on a Uniqlo t-shirt</span><span class='zh'>解码优衣库 T 恤上的混淆 Bash 脚本</span></a><span class="dom">tris.sherliker.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN. The stunt demonstrates how software-like code can slip into consumer channels via retail distribution, highlighting trust and supply-chain risks. It argues for stronger code provenance checks and verification in modern supply networks.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。这一举动揭示了软件代码通过零售渠道渗透到消费端的风险与供应链信任问题，并呼吁在现代供应链中加强代码来源核验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kimi-K3 is now available on HuggingFace, enabling researchers and developers to try, fine-tune, and benchmark the model with familiar tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi-K3 已在 HuggingFace 上公开，可供研究者和开发者直接试用与微调。</span></p>

The release underscores the ongoing push to open-source options and community collaboration around language models on popular platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi-K3 已在 HuggingFace 上公开，可供研究者和开发者直接试用与微调。此举进一步推动开源模型在 HuggingFace 生态中的扩展，便于社区贡献和基准测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apnews.com/article/john-deere-right-to-repair-agriculture-equipment-cb7514ffedb95c130a976af661f2bc02" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John Deere owners will get the right to repair equipment under FTC settlement</span><span class='zh'>FTC和解赋予约翰迪尔设备的修理权</span></a><span class="dom">apnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs.</span><span class='zh'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs. It signals a shift toward repairability by nudging OEMs to share tools, manuals, and parts, and could boost independent repair shops. This case could influence broader right-to-repair momentum in tech-enabled farming.</span><span class='zh'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。此举或促成独立修理商的兴起，或对农业科技领域的修理权运动产生示范效应。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Amazon Web Services disclosed inaccuracies in its estimated billing data, leading to charges totaling about $1.7B across customers. The incident highlights how cloud-cost visibility can fail at scale and underscores the need for robust auditing, refund mechanisms, and better tooling for cost governance in large cloud environments.</span><span class='zh'>云成本监控在大规模云环境中暴露出不足，估算账单数据存在不准确之处，涉案金额约17亿美元。此事凸显需要更强的成本可视化、审计与退款流程，并推动改进的成本管理工具与治理方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://werd.io/american-ai-is-locked-down-and-proprietary-its-losing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>China’s open-weights AI strategy is winning</span><span class='zh'>中国的开放权重AI策略正在取得胜利</span></a><span class="dom">werd.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration. This approach is gaining momentum and challenging the dominance of proprietary AI, potentially accelerating adoption across sectors and reshaping global AI competition.</span><span class='zh'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。这一路径正在获得势头，挑战以往的专有 AI 主导地位，或将加速各行业的应用并改变全球 AI 竞争格局。</span></p>

Open weights enable researchers to compare methods and stress-test safety features, potentially accelerating progress while requiring thoughtful governance.</span><span class='zh'>Inkling 是 Thinking Machines 的开源权重模型。这体现了对透明、可测试架构的推进。开源权重便于研究者对比方法、测试安全性，但也需要谨慎治理以防滥用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release. It discusses the risk of misuse and proposes criteria for when and how weights should be shared or accessed.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。文中指出若在缺乏防护的情况下公开权重可能被滥用，并提出负责任共享与准入控制的标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://videocardz.com/newz/lg-monitors-silently-install-software-through-windows-update-without-user-consent" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LG monitors silently install software through Windows Update without consent</span><span class='zh'>LG显示器通过Windows更新在未征得同意的情况下悄悄安装软件</span></a><span class="dom">videocardz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent. The bundled software can run in the background and may raise privacy and security concerns, creating a potential for bloat. Readers should review update offers, remove unwanted components, and consider blocking vendor software when possible.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。该软件可能后台运行，带来隐私与安全风险，并可能造成系统臃肿。读者应在更新时谨慎勾选、移除不需要的组件，并在可行时阻止厂商软件推送。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.opentools.studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter：开放打印机项目</span></a><span class="dom">opentools.studio</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers. The initiative could accelerate repairability, transparency, and ecosystem innovation for makers and businesses alike.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。该方向可能促进可修复性、透明度和生态创新，但也需关注可持续性与供应链问题。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts.</span><span class='zh'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A privacy-focused initiative that advocates simpler consent UX and fewer cookie banners, potentially proposing standards or tools to reduce intrusive tracking prompts. This movement aims to improve browsing experience while preserving essential privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>该计划推动更简洁的同意界面与更少的 Cookie 横幅，提出降低追踪提示的标准或工具，改善浏览体验的同时保持隐私保护。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration. They call for governance and standards instead of broad bans, highlighting that open weights accelerate safety research and tooling when responsibly managed.</span><span class='zh'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。他们呼吁以治理和标准取代全面禁令，强调在负责任管理下，开源权重能加速安全研究和工具开发。</span></p>

It weighs transparency, reader trust, and the potential impact on ranking, moderation, and author accountability.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在 Hacker News 上为 AI 生成的文章添加标记的可行性与影响。核心在于提升透明度与读者信任，同时考虑对排序、审核和作者责任的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kitsumed.github.io/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android may soon restrict on-device ADB</span><span class='zh'>Android 可能很快限制设备端 ADB</span></a><span class="dom">kitsumed.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts.</span><span class='zh'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts. The move aims to shrink the attack surface of debugging tools on Android, but may complicate automation and enterprise workflows. Expect gradual rollout across upcoming Android updates.</span><span class='zh'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。此举旨在降低调试工具的攻击面，但可能增加自动化与企业工作流的难度。相关改动将会在未来的 Android 更新中逐步落地。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/virginia-bans-sale-of-geolocation-data" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Virginia bans sale of precise geolocation data</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州禁止出售地理位置数据</span></a><span class="dom">hunton.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices.</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices. The move tightens location data markets and will impact marketers, data brokers, and app developers who rely on location signals. It also heightens regulatory fragmentation as more states consider similar restrictions.</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。此举收紧了地理位置信息市场，影响依赖位置信号的广告商、数据经纪人与应用开发者。同时，这也可能推动其他州采取类似限制，加剧合规碎片化。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='quantamagazine.org' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>For first time, a cell built from scratch grows and divides</span><span class='zh'>首次从零构建的细胞能够生长并分裂</span></a><span class="dom">quantamagazine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology.</span><span class='zh'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology. By assembling functional cellular life from individual components, the work advances the dream of programmable biology and could unlock new biotechnologies and origins-of-life insights. Yet, challenges around stability, safety, and control remain as researchers push toward more complex designs.</span><span class='zh'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。通过从单个组件组装功能性细胞生命，这项工作推动了可编程生物学的实现，并可能为生物技术和起源研究带来新机遇。当然，稳定性、安全性与可控性仍是未来进一步复杂化设计时需要面对的挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>940</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>295</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49012070" data-id="49012070" data-tags="Culture|Hardware" data-score="920" data-comments="311" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012070" data-time="1784748139">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/na_announce/status/2079952538040672302" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John C. Dvorak has died</span><span class='zh'>约翰·C·德沃拉克去世</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Veteran tech journalist John C.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Veteran tech journalist John C. Dvorak has died, leaving a controversial but influential footprint in tech media and early PC culture. His outspoken opinions and long-running columns helped shape debates around computing hardware and industry trends.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。他直言不讳的观点与长篇专栏塑造了关于计算硬件与行业趋势的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen 3.8 is the latest release in its Qwen family, aiming to improve instruction-following and response quality.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴推出 Qwen 3.8，作为 Qwen 系列的最新版本，力求提升指令遵循能力与输出质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen 3.8 is the latest release in its Qwen family, aiming to improve instruction-following and response quality. The update underscores a continued push by Chinese tech firms to scale capable LLMs for both consumer apps and enterprise use. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</span></a><span class="dom">nikolays.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</span><span class='zh'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jurassic Park computers in excruciating detail</span><span class='zh'>《侏罗纪公园》中的计算机：详尽到极致</span></a><span class="dom">fabiensanglard.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces. It reveals how the prop-makers staged computing to look convincing on 1993-era tech, shaping viewers&#x27; perception of digital authority. The analysis also explains why these UI aesthetics continue to influence cinematic design.</span><span class='zh'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。作者还讨论其对后续影视作品在呈现技术方面的影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ptrchm.com/posts/nothing-works-and-everyone-is-euphoric/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If coding has been solved, why does software keep getting worse?</span><span class='zh'>如果编码已被解决，软件为何还在变坏？</span></a><span class="dom">ptrchm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery. The piece critiques hype and explores how teams manage complexity, proposing signals for healthier practices like modularity and testing.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。批评行业热潮背后的错配，讨论如何通过模块化、测试等方式提升软件质量。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://openwrt.org/toh/openwrt/one" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://openwrt.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.22.0-20250926' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openwrt.org' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openwrt.org/toh/openwrt/one" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One – Open Hardware Router</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One：开源硬件路由器</span></a><span class="dom">openwrt.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end.</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end. The project signals a move toward verifiably open networking gear that prioritizes user sovereignty, security, and long-term repairability. It could reshape how hobbyists source and build their own routers.</span><span class='zh'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。该项目预示着向更可验证的开源网络设备转变，强调用户自主性、安全性及长期可维护性。可能改变爱好者自建路由器的选型与实现方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/read-this-before-you-buy-that-tv-streaming-stick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Read this before you buy that TV streaming stick</span><span class='zh'>买电视流媒体棒前该知道的事</span></a><span class="dom">krebsonsecurity.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage. When shopping, prioritize reputable brands, timely firmware updates, and configurable privacy settings to minimize risk.</span><span class='zh'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。选购时应关注品牌信誉、固件更新及时性以及可配置的隐私设置，以降低风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pigeonsandplanes.com/read/music-piracy-what-cd-oink-nine-inch-nails-streaming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The lost joy of music piracy</span><span class='zh'>音乐盗版的逝去乐趣</span></a><span class="dom">pigeonsandplanes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy.</span><span class='zh'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy. The piece reflects on how the pirate-era culture created camaraderie and discovery, and asks what we lose when access becomes commodified.</span><span class='zh'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。本文回顾那段盗版文化如何塑造了发现与分享的乐趣，并讨论当下更易获取的市场会让我们错过哪些宝贵的文化体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An about 7.1-magnitude earthquake struck Japan, triggering emergency alerts and shaking across affected regions.</span><span class='zh'>日本发生7.1级地震，政府发出紧急警报，震感覆盖多个区域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An about 7.1-magnitude earthquake struck Japan, triggering emergency alerts and shaking across affected regions. Authorities are assessing damage, power outages, and aftershock risks, highlighting resilience needs for infrastructure and critical facilities in disaster response planning.</span><span class='zh'>日本发生7.1级地震，政府发出紧急警报，震感覆盖多个区域。官方正在评估损毁、断电和余震风险，基础设施和关键设施的韧性成为灾后评估的核心议题。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">59</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danq.me/2026/07/09/your-app-could-have-been-a-webpage/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your &#x27;app&#x27; could have been a webpage (so I fixed it for you)</span><span class='zh'>你的“应用”其实可以是网页版本——我把它改成网页了</span></a><span class="dom">danq.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility. The post shows concrete steps to flatten typical app functionality into a responsive web UI.</span><span class='zh'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。文中给出将常见应用功能转换成响应式网页界面的具体做法。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.mikebowler.ca/2026/07/03/co2-and-decision-making/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://files.gargoylesoftware.com/images/mike_bowler.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.mikebowler.ca' data-c='#4ade80' data-tag='Health'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">60</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mikebowler.ca/2026/07/03/co2-and-decision-making/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bottleneck might be the air in the room</span><span class='zh'>瓶颈可能在房间里的空气</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mikebowler.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams.</span><span class='zh'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams. The post argues that improving ventilation and air quality is a cost-effective way to boost focus and judgment in meetings and workplaces.</span><span class='zh'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。文章强调通过改善通风和空气质量是提升专注力与判断力的成本效益办法，尤其在会议和工作场所。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/malisper/pgrust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres rewritten in Rust, now passing 100% of the Postgres regression tests</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL，现已通过 100% 回归测试</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software.</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software. While not a drop-in replacement, the effort demonstrates how safer memory management and modern tooling could influence database internals.</span><span class='zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。虽然不是直接的替代，但这项工作展示了更安全的内存管理与现代工具链对数据库内部实现的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.comaps.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CoMaps – FOSS Offline Maps</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps：开源离线地图</span></a><span class="dom">comaps.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access.</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access. It emphasizes privacy and local control by bundling downloadable map data and routing logic, avoiding reliance on centralized services. This makes it appealing for privacy-minded users and developers who want a portable, maintainable map solution.</span><span class='zh'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。它强调隐私与本地控制，提供可下载的地图数据与路由逻辑，避免对集中服务的依赖。对注重隐私的用户和开发者而言，这是一种可移植、可维护的地图方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://allaboutcookies.org/eu-mandatory-distracted-driver-system" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every new car sold in the European Union must include a driver monitoring camera</span><span class='zh'>欧盟规定所有新车必须装配驾驶员监控摄像头</span></a><span class="dom">allaboutcookies.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents. The requirement will push automakers to integrate interior cameras and analytics, raising privacy, data handling, and compliance concerns. Given the EU market&#x27;s scale, this could set a de facto global safety standard for vehicle interiors.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。厂商需在车内安装摄像头并接入分析功能，同时引发隐私保护、数据处理与合规性方面的担忧。凭借其市场规模，该举或将为全球车辆内饰安全标准定调。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry.</span><span class='zh'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry. Such claims are subjected to rigorous verification, as a verified counterexample would upend established theory.</span><span class='zh'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。此类主张需要严格验证，一旦成立将彻底改变相关理论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/HedgieMarkets/status/2081534588485296565" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI companies are shredding rare books</span><span class='zh'>AI公司正在撕碎珍稀书籍</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation. The trend highlights the tension between rapid AI development and safeguarding irreplaceable artifacts, prompting calls for governance and licensing reforms. Experts warn the losses could be irreversible if these materials are discarded before proper archival decisions are made.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。此趋势突显在推动AI高速发展时保护不可替代文献的难题，促使推动治理与许可框架的改革。若属实，损失可能是不可逆的，相关机构被敦促做出更审慎的档案保存决策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-30-stacked-pull-requests-are-now-in-public-preview/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs are now live on GitHub</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 现已上线堆叠 PR（Stacked PRs）</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview. This workflow can streamline complex changes, reduce review churn, and improve collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。该工作流有助于简化复杂改动、降低评审成本、提升协作效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bun, the fast JavaScript runtime, is being rewritten in Rust, a move aimed at boosting performance and ecosystem stability, but it also raises questions about compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 的核心将用 Rust 重写，旨在提升性能与生态稳定性，但也带来兼容性与迁移方面的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bun, the fast JavaScript runtime, is being rewritten in Rust, a move aimed at boosting performance and ecosystem stability, but it also raises questions about compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 的核心将用 Rust 重写，旨在提升性能与生态稳定性，但也带来兼容性与迁移方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://earendil.com/posts/session-portability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The session you cannot take with you</span><span class='zh'>你带不走的会话</span></a><span class="dom">earendil.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices.</span><span class='zh'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices. The piece surveys approaches like portable session tokens, device linking, and cryptographic handoffs, weighing security and UX trade-offs. The takeaway is that truly portable sessions could unlock seamless multi-device experiences.</span><span class='zh'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。文中探讨了可移植会话令牌、设备绑定与密钥交接等方案，并权衡安全性与用户体验的取舍。真正的会话可移植性或将解锁无缝的多设备使用体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber. This tiered lineup signals Google&#x27;s continued push to optimize latency, cost, and safety across use cases.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。Flash 系列强调在消费级应用和代码场景下的低延迟、低资源占用，拓展 Gemini 的适用场景。此举体现谷歌在用例分层、降低成本与提升安全性方面的持续布局。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls.</span><span class='zh'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls. This kind of passive observation highlights security and privacy implications for home networks and aerial devices, even when devices aren&#x27;t in visible sight. It also underscores practical limits like range, clutter, and legal boundaries.</span><span class='zh'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。这类被动观测引发对家庭网络和无人机安全与隐私的担忧，同时也暴露出覆盖范围、信号干扰和法律边界等局限。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.playstation.com/2026/07/01/physical-disc-production-ending-in-january-2028-for-new-games-releasing-on-playstation-consoles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Physical disc production ending in Jan 2028 for new games on PlayStation</span><span class='zh'>PlayStation 将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘生产</span></a><span class="dom">blog.playstation.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy.</span><span class='zh'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy. The move reduces manufacturing and distribution costs but may affect collectors, retailers, and regions with stronger physical markets. It signals a lasting shift in how console games are distributed and consumed.</span><span class='zh'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。此举降低了制造与发行成本，但可能影响收藏者、零售商以及实体市场较强的地区，预示着主机游戏发行与消费方式的长期转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tech.supercarblondie.com/japan-recovers-up-to-90-of-lithium-from-used-ev-batteries/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Japan develops a method to recover up to 90% of lithium from used EV batteries</span><span class='zh'>日本开发可从废旧新能源汽车电池回收高达90%的锂</span></a><span class="dom">tech.supercarblondie.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience.</span><span class='zh'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience. The advance could lower material costs and lessen environmental impact by reducing mining and refining needs.</span><span class='zh'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。此举可能降低成本、减轻采矿与精炼带来的环境压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.xbox.com/en-us/2026/07/06/resetting-xbox/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Resetting Xbox</span><span class='zh'>Xbox 重置</span></a><span class="dom">news.xbox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased.</span><span class='zh'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased. The guidance aims to give players a reliable recovery path for performance issues, software glitches, or corrupted saves. The update underscores the importance of predictable recovery flows as consoles grow more complex.</span><span class='zh'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。该指南为应对性能问题、软件故障或数据损坏提供可靠的恢复路径。随着主机软件日益复杂，可靠的恢复流程显得尤为重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices.</span><span class='zh'>KOReader 依然是一款强大的跨平台开源电子书阅读器，支持多种格式与设备，包括电子墨水设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://wangcong.org/2026-06-30-why-i-stopped-arguing-with-people.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Most arguments are about ego, not ideas</span><span class='zh'>大多數爭論源於自尊心，而非觀點</span></a><span class="dom">wangcong.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas.</span><span class='zh'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas. The piece argues for stepping away from heated exchanges and focusing on listening and shared goals, which can make discussions more productive and informative for tech communities.</span><span class='zh'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。文章倡導在情緒升溫時停止爭辯，專注傾聽並明確共同目標，讓技術社群的討論更高效、具啟發性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/47/1/zsad253/7280269" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sleep regularity is a stronger predictor of mortality risk than sleep duration (2023)</span><span class='zh'>睡眠规律性比睡眠时长更能预测死亡风险</span></a><span class="dom">academic.oup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration. Using long-term wearable data, the study links consistent sleep timing with better health outcomes, independent of hours slept. The finding shifts emphasis toward regular schedules as a health strategy and dovetails with consumer wellness tech.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。通过长期可穿戴设备数据，研究发现稳定的睡眠时序与更好的健康结果相关，且与实际睡眠时长无关。该发现将健康策略的重点从睡眠时长转向规律作息，并与可穿戴健康科技的发展相吻合。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways.</span><span class='zh'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways. It highlights typography’s impact on AI text detectors and reveals current recognition limits.</span><span class='zh'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。它揭示排版对 AI 文本检测的影响与当前识别能力的边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.risky.biz/risky-bulletin-hacker-wipes-romanias-entire-land-registry-database/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hacker wipes Romania&#x27;s land registry database</span><span class='zh'>黑客抹除罗马尼亚土地登记数据库</span></a><span class="dom">news.risky.biz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions.</span><span class='zh'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions. The incident highlights catastrophic risks from insufficient backups, disaster recovery planning, and the high stakes of protecting centralized public registries.</span><span class='zh'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。此事凸显了集中式公共登记在备份、灾难恢复和安全保护不足时会带来的严重风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</span></a><span class="dom">systima.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-27b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bonsai 27B: A 27B-Class model that runs on a phone</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B：能在手机上运行的27B级模型</span></a><span class="dom">prismml.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone.</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone. Achieving on-device inference requires aggressive optimization (pruning, quantization, efficient runtimes) to fit memory and power constraints, but it promises lower latency and stronger privacy. This shift hints at a future where powerful AI can operate without constant cloud access.</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。实现本地推理需要对模型进行大幅优化（裁剪、量化、高效运行时），以适应内存与电池限制，但可带来更低的延迟和更强的隐私保护。这预示着强大AI有望在不依赖云端的情况下运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techradar.com/vpn/vpn-privacy-security/vpns-are-lawful-technical-tools-says-eu-court-in-landmark-anne-frank-copyright-ruling" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&#x27;VPNs are lawful technical tools,&#x27; says EU Court in landmark copyright ruling</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法技术工具</span></a><span class="dom">techradar.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information. This ruling clarifies that circumvention or privacy protections are not inherently illegal, shaping how copyright enforcement and online freedom interplay. It signals a supportive stance for privacy-preserving tech in Europe.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。该判决澄清了 VPN 使用本身并非非法，对提供商与用户均有积极影响，同时为欧洲的版权执法与互联网自由定调。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://javoriuski.com/post/youtube" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Leaking YouTube creators&#x27; private videos</span><span class='zh'>泄露 YouTube 创作者的私人视频</span></a><span class="dom">javoriuski.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content.</span><span class='zh'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content. The incident spotlights the friction between creator workflows, platform safeguards, and third-party storage or collaboration tools. It reinforces the need for strict access auditing, multi-factor authentication, and encrypted pipelines in large creator ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。事件凸显了创作者工作流、平台防护与第三方存储/协作工具之间的矛盾。需要加强访问审计、启用多因素认证，以及在大型创作者生态系统中实施端到端加密和更严格的数据管控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</span><span class='zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</span></a><span class="dom">popcar.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑 AI 研究室是否以引人注目的扩张和炒作来取代稳健的安全与长期研究，并以“Pelicanmaxxing”作为隐喻。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techdirt.com/2026/07/15/sony-deletes-a-bunch-more-movies-from-the-accounts-of-people-who-bought-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sony deletes more movies from the accounts of people who ‘bought’ them</span><span class='zh'>索尼再次从购买者账户中删除多部电影</span></a><span class="dom">techdirt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony has removed additional titles from users’ digital libraries after purchase, prompting questions about ownership, DRM, and what it means to &#x27;own&#x27; digital media.</span><span class='zh'>索尼再次从已购买用户的数字库中删除多部电影，引发关于数字所有权、DRM 与所有权概念的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony has removed additional titles from users’ digital libraries after purchase, prompting questions about ownership, DRM, and what it means to &#x27;own&#x27; digital media. The incident spotlights ongoing tensions in rights, licensing, and user trust.</span><span class='zh'>索尼再次从已购买用户的数字库中删除多部电影，引发关于数字所有权、DRM 与所有权概念的讨论。此事凸显版权、许可与用户信任之间的张力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jelly UI experiments with soft-body physics applied to native HTML form controls, delivering more tactile interactions without heavy dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>Jelly UI 将软体物理应用到原生 HTML 表单控件，带来更具触感的交互，且无需引入较重的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lji.org/news-events/news/post/new-hiv-vaccine-shows-unprecedented-success-in-preclinical-study/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New HIV vaccine shows unprecedented success in preclinical study</span><span class='zh'>新型HIV疫苗在前临床研究中取得前所未有的成功</span></a><span class="dom">lji.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough.</span><span class='zh'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough. However, preclinical success does not guarantee human efficacy; several hurdles remain. If replicated in early human trials, it could reshape HIV vaccine development and open new avenues for immunogen design.</span><span class='zh'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。需要注意的是，前临床结果并不保证在人类身上也有效，仍有多项挑战待解。若在早期人体试验中得到证实，这一突破可能推动疫苗设计的进一步突破，并改变相关研究方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/24/nvidia-microsoft-meta-open-weight-ai-models.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia, Microsoft, Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告勿过度监管开放权重模型</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems. They advocate targeted, risk-based oversight rather than broad bans, stressing practical governance over blanket prohibitions.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。他们主张以风险为基础的、有针对性的监管，而非全面禁令，强调治理应兼顾现实世界的应用场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ayush.digital/blog/the-memory-heist" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I tricked Claude into leaking your deepest, darkest secrets</span><span class='zh'>我如何骗过 Claude 让它泄露你最深的秘密</span></a><span class="dom">ayush.digital</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A crafted prompt demonstrates a vulnerability in Claude&#x27;s handling of sensitive data, revealing how advanced LLMs can leak information under certain prompts.</span><span class='zh'>通过巧妙的提示演示，揭示 Claude 在特定输入下可能泄露敏感信息的风险，凸显数据隐私与模型防护的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A crafted prompt demonstrates a vulnerability in Claude&#x27;s handling of sensitive data, revealing how advanced LLMs can leak information under certain prompts. The post underscores the data-privacy risk and the importance of guardrails, data governance, and model hardening.</span><span class='zh'>通过巧妙的提示演示，揭示 Claude 在特定输入下可能泄露敏感信息的风险，凸显数据隐私与模型防护的重要性。文章提醒开发者加强 guardrails、数据治理和对模型的安全强化。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams.</span><span class='zh'>仅依赖 Substack 容易受制于平台变动，拥有个人网站能为创作者带来更多控制权、搜索流量和收入来源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EU Parliament gave initial approval to the Chat Control proposal, advancing it to the next legislative stage.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会对‘聊天控制’提案完成初步表决，推进至下一轮立法程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU Parliament gave initial approval to the Chat Control proposal, advancing it to the next legislative stage. If enacted, it would mandate scanning private messages for abuse material, raising debates about end-to-end encryption, privacy, and platform obligations.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会对‘聊天控制’提案完成初步表决，推进至下一轮立法程序。若最终通过，可能要求对私人消息进行扫描以发现虐待材料，引发对端对端加密、隐私保护及平台责任的广泛讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://30papers.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>30papers.com – Ilya&#x27;s 30 essential ML papers, in a beginner friendly format</span><span class='zh'>30papers.com——Ilya 的 30 篇核心 ML 论文，面向初学者</span></a><span class="dom">30papers.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/10/new-york-city-deceptive-subscriptions-ban" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New York City to ban deceptive subscription practices</span><span class='zh'>纽约市将禁止欺骗性订阅行为</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges. The move aims to improve consumer protection and force clearer disclosures from subscription-based services, potentially reshaping how onboarding and renewal terms are presented online. This could raise the bar for startups and platforms relying on subscription models.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。此举将促使订阅型服务在用户注册与续订条款上提供更清晰的披露，或对采用订阅模式的企业带来影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://reclaimthenet.org/sony-deletes-551-studiocanal-movies-playstation-owners-paid-for" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sony Deletes 551 Movies PlayStation Owners Paid For</span><span class='zh'>索尼删除了 PlayStation 用户购买的 551 部电影</span></a><span class="dom">reclaimthenet.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films.</span><span class='zh'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films. The incident underlines how digital ownership hinges on licensing and can disappear with rights changes. It raises questions about the permanence of digital libraries and consumer protections.</span><span class='zh'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。事件凸显数字所有权依赖于许可，权利变更就可能导致内容消失。引发对数字馆藏永久性与消费者保护的思考。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project uses the Fable language to turn a reMarkable tablet into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter, blending code with a literary interface.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的项目利用 Fable 将 reMarkable 平板打造成《哈利波特》里汤姆·里德尔的日记界面，结合编程与叙事界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful project uses the Fable language to turn a reMarkable tablet into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter, blending code with a literary interface. It demonstrates how a programmable notebook can host narrative-driven experiences on niche hardware and invites creative experimentation with data-binding and UI.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的项目利用 Fable 将 reMarkable 平板打造成《哈利波特》里汤姆·里德尔的日记界面，结合编程与叙事界面。它展示了可编程笔记本在小众硬件上实现叙事体验的潜力，并鼓励在数据绑定与用户界面方面进行创意探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/gemini-robotics-2-brings-whole-body-intelligence-to-robots/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 brings whole body intelligence to robots</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 将整身智能引入机器人</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body. It leverages large-language-model-driven planning to orchestrate multi-limb manipulation and locomotion, enabling more flexible, goal-driven behavior in unstructured environments. This marks a step toward robots that can reason about tasks at a higher level rather than relying on hand-built controllers.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。它利用基于大模型的规划来统一多肢的动作与移动，使机器人在非结构化环境中更具灵活性与目标导向。标志着让机器人以更高层次的任务推理来完成工作的一步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/marcelroed/gigatoken/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GigaToken: ~1000x faster Language model tokenization</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken：语言模型分词速度提升约 1000 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments.</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments. If practical, it could shift cost and performance considerations for AI products that rely on tokenization as a bottleneck.</span><span class='zh'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。若落地可行，将改变依赖分词的 AI 产品的成本与性能权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/ai-s-top-startups-are-barely-publishing-their-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI&#x27;s top startups are barely publishing their research</span><span class='zh'>AI 的顶尖初创几乎不公开研究成果</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases.</span><span class='zh'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases. This sparsity raises concerns about reproducibility, safety, and how quick advances are validated, as powerful models race ahead.</span><span class='zh'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。研究不透明可能影响结果复现与安全评估，也让快速迭代中的能力提升难以被独立验证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gamingonlinux.com/2026/07/valve-open-source-the-steam-machine-e-ink-screen-so-you-can-make-your-own/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Valve open-source the Steam Machine e-ink screen so you can make your own</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏开源，教你自制</span></a><span class="dom">gamingonlinux.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects. This openness lowers barriers for hardware experimentation and highlights a community driven approach to hardware prototyping.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。这一开源举措降低了硬件试验门槛，凸显硬件原型设计的社区驱动性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI outlines how GPT-5.6 boosts price-performance through efficiency improvements and lower per-token costs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。</span></p>

The upgrade could enable cheaper, faster AI inference for developers and businesses, widening access to advanced models.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。这一升级有望让开发者和企业以更低成本获得更快的推理能力，进一步普及高阶模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yc-software/qm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>qm – Multiplayer agent harness for work</span><span class='zh'>qm：面向工作场景的多人代理框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex-security" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex Security</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling. The project provides tooling for auditing prompts, monitoring outputs, and identifying vulnerabilities across code-generation pipelines. By open-sourcing it, OpenAI accelerates community-driven security research and invites broader scrutiny of AI-enabled coding systems.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。该项目支持对 prompts、输出和代码生成流程中的潜在漏洞进行分析，并提升对 AI 辅助编码系统的可审计性。此举可能加速社区在 AI 安全领域的研究与防护工具普及。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://petapixel.com/2026/07/16/mayor-mamdani-says-landlords-cant-secretly-use-ai-images-to-advertise-properties/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NYC may require landlords and realtors to disclose the use of AI in listings</span><span class='zh'>纽约市可能要求房东披露在房源信息中使用 AI 的情况</span></a><span class="dom">petapixel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings. The move aims to curb deceptive advertising and increase transparency in online property listings, with implications for proptech platforms and compliance burdens.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。此举旨在提升在线房源广告的透明度，影响房地产科技平台的合规成本与运营方式。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">130</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.maths.cam.ac.uk/features/professor-hannah-fry-wins-leelavati-prize" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry Wins the Leelavati Prize in 2026 for Mathematics Outreach</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱获2026年 Leelavati 数学传播奖</span></a><span class="dom">maths.cam.ac.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content.</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content. The award underscores the value of science communication in expanding participation in STEM and inspires broader engagement with mathematical ideas.</span><span class='zh'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。该奖项强调了公众沟通在扩大 STEM 参与度方面的重要性，并激励更多人关注与学习数学。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>581</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49043724' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>109</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48957779" data-id="48957779" data-tags="AI" data-score="580" data-comments="380" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48957779" data-time="1784379652">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://old.reddit.com/r/math/comments/1uxj3cy/after_openais_cdc_proof_announcement_gpt56_used_a/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 used a prompt to close a 30-year gap in convex optimization</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 用提示弥补凸优化三十年难题</span></a><span class="dom">old.reddit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement. If verified, it demonstrates how carefully crafted prompts can guide AI to tackle deep mathematical problems and produce testable steps. The claim invites scrutiny of reproducibility and the proper role of AI in formal proofs.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。若属实，表明大型语言模型在帮助人类数学家进行问题表述和证据推导方面具有潜在价值，同时也需要对可重复性与独立验证保持警惕。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tedium.co/2026/07/01/online-web-forums-retrospective/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bring back crappy forums</span><span class='zh'>让糟糕论坛卷土重来</span></a><span class="dom">tedium.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation.</span><span class='zh'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation. It examines what early forums did well and what modern networks could borrow, from lightweight threading to signal-to-noise management.</span><span class='zh'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。它讨论早期论坛的优点，以及现代社群应借鉴的经验，如简化的主题讨论与信号噪声管理。</span></p>
