<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。文章强调苹果的策略改变了用户预期和生态格局，迫使厂商在整合与保留车辆专属控制间寻找平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ft.com/content/3a023b95-66c3-41e1-b0ce-df752a499541" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX bond worth 10% less than issue price – heading for junk bond status</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 债券发行价下跌 10%：有望走向垃圾债评级</span></a><span class="dom">ft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk. If the debt slides toward junk status, SpaceX could face higher refinancing costs and tighter liquidity windows as macro headwinds bite tech funding. This serves as a reminder that even highly popular tech issuers are not immune to debt-market sentiment.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。若债务评级进一步恶化，再融资成本将上升，流动性可能收紧，凸显当前宏观环境对科技股相关债务的冲击。此事再次印证即使是备受关注的科技发行也会受债市情绪影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think. Adoption friction could hobble the technology unless UX and recovery flows are rethought.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。若不改进 UX 与恢复流程，普及应用将面临更大阻力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 的进展包括内核改进、对 Apple Silicon 的更广泛支持以及性能优化，旨在让在 Mac 上运行 Linux 更加顺畅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frontline nurses report that AI decision-support and surveillance systems add administrative burden and impair patient interaction, potentially harming care quality.</span><span class='zh'>一线护士表示人工智能决策支持和工作场所监控增加了工作负担，影响与病人互动，可能损及护理质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frontline nurses report that AI decision-support and surveillance systems add administrative burden and impair patient interaction, potentially harming care quality. The piece underscores calls for better governance, transparency, and careful deployment of automation in healthcare.</span><span class='zh'>一线护士表示人工智能决策支持和工作场所监控增加了工作负担，影响与病人互动，可能损及护理质量。文章强调需要更好的治理、透明度，以及在医疗领域更谨慎地部署自动化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/moonshine-ai/moonshine/tree/main/micro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Speech Recognition and TTS in less than 500kb</span><span class='zh'>不到500KB的語音識別與文字轉語音</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices.</span><span class='zh'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices. The project highlights design choices that trade off accuracy for size, with potential uses in privacy-preserving, edge AI.</span><span class='zh'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。該項目展示了為了縮小尺寸而進行的設計取捨，對注重隱私的邊緣AI場景尤具吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcgamer.com/gaming-industry/open-source-game-engine-godot-will-no-longer-accept-ai-authored-code-contributions-we-cant-trust-heavy-users-of-ai-to-understand-their-code-enough-to-fix-it/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Godot will no longer accept AI-authored code contributions</span><span class='zh'>Godot 将不再接受 AI 编写的代码贡献</span></a><span class="dom">pcgamer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability.</span><span class='zh'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability. The policy underscores the ongoing tension in OSS between AI-assisted tooling and the need for human oversight in critical code.</span><span class='zh'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。此举凸显开源社区在 AI 辅助编码和人类审阅之间的持续张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://19thnews.org/2026/07/noaa-climate-data-website/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Former NOAA employees built Climate.us to preserve climate data and resources</span><span class='zh'>前 NOAA 员工创建 Climate.us 以保存气候数据与资源</span></a><span class="dom">19thnews.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project by former NOAA staff to archive climate data and related resources, addressing data accessibility and preservation challenges as data grows and policies evolve.</span><span class='zh'>由前 NOAA 员工创建的 Climate.us 项目，旨在存档气候数据及相关资源，解决数据日益增长与政策变化带来的获取与长期保存挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jdan.github.io/98.css/#status-bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>98.css</span><span class='zh'>98.css：让网页重现 Windows 98 风格的 CSS 框架</span></a><span class="dom">jdan.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML.</span><span class='zh'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML. It enables quick retro-style demos and nostalgia-driven UI experiments without heavy dependencies. The project showcases how small CSS kits can deliver a complete desktop-like aesthetic with just a few lines.</span><span class='zh'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。它便于快速做出复古风格的仪表板或演示，而无需繁重的依赖。该项目展示了极简 CSS 组件也能实现桌面级美观的效果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/security/chrome-stronger-with-every-update/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google fixed more Chrome bugs in June than over the past two years, thanks to AI</span><span class='zh'>谷歌靠 AI，在六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年总和</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌称借助 AI 的分流与补丁生成，六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年的总和。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined. This shows how AI tooling can accelerate vulnerability response and elevate browser hardening. It could foreshadow broader shifts in security workflows across software teams.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌称借助 AI 的分流与补丁生成，六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年的总和。这显示出 AI 工具能加速漏洞响应、提升浏览器安全性。此举或预示软件团队在安全工作流程上的更广泛变革。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mynintendonews.com/2026/06/26/nintendo-has-raised-its-employees-base-salary-by-10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nintendo has raised its employees base salary by 10%</span><span class='zh'>任天堂将员工基本薪资提高10%</span></a><span class="dom">mynintendonews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company. The move may aid retention and morale amid tight labor markets while squeezing margins modestly. It also mirrors broader wage-tightening and inflation-adaptation trends in the industry.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。此举有助于提升留任与士气，但可能对利润率产生一定挤压，反映出行业的薪资调整与通胀应对趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Switzerland has 25 gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>自由市场的谬误：为何瑞士有25 Gbit互联网而美国没有</span></a><span class="dom">stefan.schueller.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market. It contrasts that with structural barriers in the U.S.—incumbent incentives, pole and rights-of-way issues, and regulatory choices—that have kept speeds and deployment lagging despite market rhetoric.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。与之相对，美国因既得利益、线路与通行权问题及监管选择，导致部署和速率长期落后。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>534</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>429</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48763035" data-id="48763035" data-tags="Security" data-score="531" data-comments="222" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035" data-time="1783005916">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@iblech/116769502749142438" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Since Linux 6.9, LUKS suspend stopped wiping disk-encryption keys from memory</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存擦除磁盘加密密钥</span></a><span class="dom">mathstodon.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume.</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume. The decision highlights the ongoing tension between safeguarding encryption keys and preserving usability in sleep states.</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。这一取舍凸显了在安全性与可用性之间的持续权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cy4kdd1e0ejo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>First atmosphere found on Earth-like planet in habitable zone of distant star</span><span class='zh'>在遥远恒星的宜居带首次发现地球样行星的大气</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system. The finding invigorates plans for future telescopes to search for biosignatures and assess habitability on exoplanets.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。此发现将推动未来望远镜在星际尺度上寻找生物征兆并评估宜居性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/jul/24/openai-rogue-hacker" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Be skeptical of OpenAI&#x27;s rogue hacker agent story</span><span class='zh'>对 OpenAI 的 rogue hacker agent 故事保持怀疑</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Questioning the veracity of a reported OpenAI rogue hacker agent incident, the piece argues for skepticism and better verification in AI security reporting.</span><span class='zh'>文章对关于 OpenAI 失控黑客代理的报道保持怀疑，强调需要更严格的核验与背景信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Questioning the veracity of a reported OpenAI rogue hacker agent incident, the piece argues for skepticism and better verification in AI security reporting. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</span><span class='zh'>卖出2500个 MIDI 记录器让我明白硬件并不难</span></a><span class="dom">chipweinberger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://noma.security/blog/gitlost-how-we-tricked-githubs-ai-agent-into-leaking-private-repos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitLost: We Tricked GitHub&#x27;s AI Agent into Leaking Private Repos</span><span class='zh'>GitLost：我们欺骗 GitHub 的 AI 代理泄露私有仓库</span></a><span class="dom">noma.security</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories. By manipulating prompts and data flows, they demonstrate leakage risks from copilots and automated tooling. This underscores the need for stronger data-handling controls, better prompt safeguards, and auditability in AI-assisted development ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。通过操纵提示和数据流，他们演示了协作工具与自动化流程中的数据泄露风险。这凸显了在 AI 辅助开发生态中加强数据处理、提示防护和审计机制的重要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://righttointelligence.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='righttointelligence.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://righttointelligence.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protect your right to run local AI</span><span class='zh'>保护本地运行 AI 的权利</span></a><span class="dom">righttointelligence.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence.</span><span class='zh'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence. That shift would boost privacy, data control, and resilience, though hardware costs and licensing remain barriers. The piece frames why this matters to developers and enthusiasts who want autonomy over their AI tools.</span><span class='zh'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。这一转变能提升隐私、数据掌控和系统韧性，但硬件成本和许可证限制仍是现实障碍。文章聚焦对于希望掌控 AI 工具的开发者和爱好者为何重要。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/04d1d1e4-bc75-476a-97cf-49055cd98d31/cdc_proof.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cdn.openai.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cdn.openai.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/04d1d1e4-bc75-476a-97cf-49055cd98d31/cdc_proof.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra produces proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 产出循环双覆盖猜想的证明（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">cdn.openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided. If verified, this would mark a notable milestone in AI-assisted formal proofs and could accelerate future mathematical work, but the proof will be rigorously scrutinized by the community. The episode highlights AI&#x27;s potential to aid deep mathematics while underscoring the need for careful validation.</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。若经同行验证，这一成果可能标志着 AI 辅助形式化证明迈出重要一步，或加速后续的数学研究，但证明将接受严格审查。此事凸显 AI 在高深数学领域的潜力，同时也强调需要谨慎验证。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.gingerbill.org/article/2026/07/10/good-tools-are-invisible/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f472b6' data-tag='Design'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gingerbill.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gingerbill.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">174</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gingerbill.org/article/2026/07/10/good-tools-are-invisible/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Good Tools Are Invisible</span><span class='zh'>好工具往往无形</span></a><span class="dom">gingerbill.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Great tools fade into the background by design, reducing cognitive load and letting people focus on what matters.</span><span class='zh'>优秀的工具被设计为在工作流中隐形，降低认知负担，让人专注于核心任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Great tools fade into the background by design, reducing cognitive load and letting people focus on what matters. The piece cautions that invisibility should not hide value from teams and argues for clear outcomes and good integration.</span><span class='zh'>优秀的工具被设计为在工作流中隐形，降低认知负担，让人专注于核心任务。文章提醒不要让它们的价值被忽视，并强调要有清晰的成效与良好的集成。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>523</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858121' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>238</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48977711" data-id="48977711" data-tags="Privacy|Politics|Legal|Security" data-score="521" data-comments="318" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977711" data-time="1784549648">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edri.org/our-work/the-eu-is-about-to-sell-our-most-sensitive-data-to-the-us-for-visa-free-travel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://edri.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/2026_1Artboard-7.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edri.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">175</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edri.org/our-work/the-eu-is-about-to-sell-our-most-sensitive-data-to-the-us-for-visa-free-travel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The EU is about to sell our most sensitive data to the US for visa-free travel</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将把最敏感数据共享给美国以实现免签旅行</span></a><span class="dom">edri.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel. The plan raises privacy, data protection, and oversight concerns about transatlantic data flows and potential surveillance risks. It highlights tensions between security goals and GDPR-era protections, and may push regulators to tighten controls on data access and purpose limitation.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。相关安排可能加剧对监控与数据使用边界的质疑，并促使监管机构就数据访问权限和用途限制加强把关。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://software.annas-archive.gl/AnnaArchivist/annas-archive/-/work_items/234" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Books (or similar) all book scans – $200k bounty (2025)</span><span class='zh'>谷歌图书（或同类）对完整书籍扫描的20万美元悬赏（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">software.annas-archive.gl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books.</span><span class='zh'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books. This initiative could accelerate digitization, OCR accuracy, and AI training data availability, while raising licensing, copyright, and provenance questions. Expect debates over public-domain scope, fair use, and the balance between accessibility and rights.</span><span class='zh'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。此举可能加速书籍的数字化、光学字符识别的准确性，以及可供机器学习训练的数据集，但也引发版权、授权与来源等问题。公众领域、使用权与可获取性的平衡将成为讨论焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pradyuprasad.com/writings/how-to-ask-for-help/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How to ask for help from people who don&#x27;t know you</span><span class='zh'>如何向不认识你的人寻求帮助</span></a><span class="dom">pradyuprasad.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes.</span><span class='zh'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes. The guidance emphasizes clear, respectful asks and the strategic use of warm introductions to overcome friction.</span><span class='zh'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。文章给出一系列实用策略：简短有力的表述、附带背景信息、以及便于对方快速答复的具体问题。把对方从“陌生人”变成愿意帮助的对象，核心在于高效沟通和善用引介。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.artfish.ai/p/offloading-thinking-to-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Are we offloading too much of our thinking to AI?</span><span class='zh'>我们是不是把太多思考交给 AI？</span></a><span class="dom">artfish.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency.</span><span class='zh'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency. The piece calls for balancing automation with human judgment.</span><span class='zh'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。应在自动化与人类判断之间保持平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for.</span><span class='zh'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for. The piece argues for richer metrics and stress testing to avoid overestimating performance in real-world use.</span><span class='zh'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。98% 这样的数字若忽略基线、样本偏差或边缘案例，容易带来错误解读。建议采用更全面的指标和鲁棒性测试来评估真实世界表现。</span></p>

It outlines potential gains in memory safety, binary size, and cross-platform portability while acknowledging migration complexity.</span><span class='zh'>本文更新了将 Rust 代码库改写为 Zig 的进展，介绍关键设计取舍、初步成果与尚待解决的挑战。讨论了在内存安全、二进制大小和跨平台可移植性方面的潜在收益，同时也强调迁移的复杂性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://box2d.org/posts/2026/06/announcing-box3d/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Box3D, an open source 3D physics engine</span><span class='zh'>宣布发布 Box3D：开源的 3D 物理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">box2d.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage.</span><span class='zh'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage. It aims to deliver robust rigid-body dynamics with a modular design and easier integration for games and simulations, inviting community contributions. If you build 3D physics into your project, Box3D could become a go-to option without licensing friction.</span><span class='zh'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。若你的项目需要 3D 物理，这个引擎可能成为无许可负担的首选选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/06/the-post-covid-decline-in-the-labor-share/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The labor share of income in the US is at its lowest post-war level</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动收入份额降至战后最低水平</span></a><span class="dom">libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output.</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output. The trend implies slower wage growth for workers and potential pressure on consumer demand, with implications for inequality and policy debates. Analysts see a shift in income distribution driven by automation, globalization, and pricing power.</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。此趋势意味着劳动者工资增长可能放缓，消费力受压，且可能加剧收入不平等。分析师将关注结构性因素如何影响工资与投资回报的长期分配。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/crime-pays-the-egg-bandits-made-a" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Egg Bandits Made a Thousand Times the Fine They Just Paid for Price Fixing</span><span class='zh'>蛋价共谋者缴纳的罚款远不及其收益</span></a><span class="dom">thebignewsletter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme.</span><span class='zh'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme. This piece argues for tougher penalties, and possibly criminal charges, to deter cartels and protect consumers.</span><span class='zh'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。文章强调只有提高处罚力度、甚至引入刑事责任，才能真正抑制此类共谋。</span></p>

The move signals a push toward more capable AI coding assistants and longer, more coherent interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi 发布 K3-256k 模型，具备更长的上下文容量，适合处理更长的代码和推理对话。这反映出在代码助手领域追求更强大的对话连贯性和可处理任务的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/2026/07/20/human-mathematicians-are-being-outcounterexampled/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Human mathematicians are being outcounterexampled</span><span class='zh'>人类数学家正被反例发现所超越</span></a><span class="dom">xenaproject.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task. This shift reshapes collaboration between mathematicians and machines, emphasizing verification, reproducibility, and new roles for intuition in research. The trend could accelerate discovery but also demands careful framing of proofs and trust in automated reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。这种变化正在重新定义数学家与机器之间的协作，强调验证、可重复性，以及直觉在研究中的新角色。此趋势可能加速发现，但也需要对证明与自动推理的信任进行审慎权衡。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>486</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>249</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48883343" data-id="48883343" data-tags="AI" data-score="486" data-comments="318" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883343" data-time="1783881116">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice.</span><span class='zh'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice. It helps practitioners diagnose performance bottlenecks beyond surface readings. Includes tips on CPU/memory usage, process states, and how sampling affects observations.</span><span class='zh'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。提供实用建议，帮助读者理解 CPU/内存、进程状态和 I/O 等数据在监控中的含义及刷新对观察的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral unveils Robostral Navigate, a state-of-the-art robotics navigation model designed to improve real-time planning, obstacle avoidance, and multi-sensor fusion.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral 发布 Robostral Navigate，这是一款前沿的机器人导航模型，聚焦实时规划、避障和多传感器融合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral unveils Robostral Navigate, a state-of-the-art robotics navigation model designed to improve real-time planning, obstacle avoidance, and multi-sensor fusion. Early demonstrations suggest stronger robustness and efficiency in dynamic environments, with broad potential for logistics and service robotics.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral 发布 Robostral Navigate，这是一款前沿的机器人导航模型，聚焦实时规划、避障和多传感器融合。早期演示显示在动态环境中具备更强鲁棒性与效率，未来可能推动物流、服务机器人等领域。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content.</span><span class='zh'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content. The piece emphasizes rigor, citations, and nuance as defining traits that distinguish quality writing from AI churn.</span><span class='zh'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。作者强调严格性、引文与语义深度，指出这些是区分高质量写作与 AI 产出差异的关键。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open-source AI platforms are maturing, with new models, licensing debates, and governance challenges shaping the ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>开源 AI 正在走向成熟，新的模型、许可与治理挑战共同塑造生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open-source AI platforms are maturing, with new models, licensing debates, and governance challenges shaping the ecosystem. The piece benchmarks how open tooling stacks up against proprietary options and why safety, transparency, and community norms matter for long-term viability.</span><span class='zh'>开源 AI 正在走向成熟，新的模型、许可与治理挑战共同塑造生态。相较于商业垄断，开放工具链在安全性、透明度和社区规范方面的表现将决定其长期可用性与影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/28058" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex starts encrypting sub-agent prompts</span><span class='zh'>Codex 开始对子代理提示进行加密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI Codex now encrypts prompts used by sub-agents to protect sensitive instructions across agent calls.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Codex 现对子代理所使用的提示进行加密，以在代理调用之间保护敏感指令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI Codex now encrypts prompts used by sub-agents to protect sensitive instructions across agent calls. The change reduces prompt leakage and improves security for multi-agent workflows, though it may complicate debugging and require key management.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Codex 现对子代理所使用的提示进行加密，以在代理调用之间保护敏感指令。此举降低了提示泄露风险，提升多代理工作流的安全性，但可能增加调试难度与密钥管理负担。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jamesob/local-llm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jamesob&#x27;s guide to running SOTA LLMs locally</span><span class='zh'>Jamesob 的本地运行 SOTA 大语言模型指南</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical guide to running state of the art LLMs on local hardware.</span><span class='zh'>这是一份在本地硬件上运行 SOTA 大语言模型的实用指南，涵盖模型选择、量化、离载与工具链等方面的内存和算力优化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical guide to running state of the art LLMs on local hardware. It covers model selection, quantization, offloading, and tooling to optimize memory and compute, enabling researchers to prototype without cloud dependence while noting tradeoffs in accuracy and resource use.</span><span class='zh'>这是一份在本地硬件上运行 SOTA 大语言模型的实用指南，涵盖模型选择、量化、离载与工具链等方面的内存和算力优化。帮助研究者在不依赖云端的情况下进行原型开发，同时指明精度与资源成本之间的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FractalFir/crustc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>crustc: entirety of `rustc`, translated to C</span><span class='zh'>crustc：将整个 rustc 翻译为 C</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>crustc is an ambitious project that translates the entirety of the Rust compiler into C, aiming to make rustc more portable and easier to bootstrap on unusual toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>crustc 是个雄心勃勃的工程，把整个 Rust 编译器翻译成 C，目标是提升 rustc 在非常规工具链上的可移植性和引导能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>crustc is an ambitious project that translates the entirety of the Rust compiler into C, aiming to make rustc more portable and easier to bootstrap on unusual toolchains. The repo is experimental but useful as a study in compiler architecture, portability trade-offs, and alternate bootstrapping strategies.</span><span class='zh'>crustc 是个雄心勃勃的工程，把整个 Rust 编译器翻译成 C，目标是提升 rustc 在非常规工具链上的可移植性和引导能力。该项目处于实验性阶段，但对研究编译器架构、可移植性权衡以及替代引导策略很有价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/wsff.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Software Factories Fail (or: harness engineering is not enough)</span><span class='zh'>为什么软件工厂会失败（或：仅靠工程化不足以成事）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that software factories fail not because of tech per se but due to overreliance on harness engineering and process alone.</span><span class='zh'>本文指出，仅靠工程化流程难以解决 AI 驱动工作流的复杂性，需要更广义的上下文工程、治理和守则，才能实现可可靠扩展的开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that software factories fail not because of tech per se but due to overreliance on harness engineering and process alone. The piece suggests you need broader context engineering, governance, and guardrails to build reliable AI-enabled software.</span><span class='zh'>本文指出，仅靠工程化流程难以解决 AI 驱动工作流的复杂性，需要更广义的上下文工程、治理和守则，才能实现可可靠扩展的开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/30364" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.5 Codex reasoning-token clustering may be leading to degraded performance</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.5 Codex 推理令牌聚类可能导致性能下降</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers report that clustering reasoning tokens in GPT-5.5 Codex-style workflows may degrade performance, suggesting a misalignment between token organization and inference efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>研究指出在 GPT-5.5/Codex 风格工作流中对推理令牌进行聚类可能降低性能，表明令牌组织方式与推理效率之间存在错配。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers report that clustering reasoning tokens in GPT-5.5 Codex-style workflows may degrade performance, suggesting a misalignment between token organization and inference efficiency. The finding has implications for how developers architect long-chain reasoning or tool-assisted prompts in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>研究指出在 GPT-5.5/Codex 风格工作流中对推理令牌进行聚类可能降低性能，表明令牌组织方式与推理效率之间存在错配。这一发现对在生产系统中设计长链推理或工具辅助提示的架构设计具有重要意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/Codeberg/org/pulls/1254" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codeberg Bans Cryptocurrency Projects</span><span class='zh'>Codeberg 禁止加密货币项目</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codeberg updates its policy to ban cryptocurrency-related projects, citing legal, ethical, and security concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Codeberg 更新政策，禁止在平台上托管与加密货币相关的项目，理由涉及法律、伦理与安全等风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codeberg updates its policy to ban cryptocurrency-related projects, citing legal, ethical, and security concerns. The move highlights how open-source hosting platforms balance openness with risk management.</span><span class='zh'>Codeberg 更新政策，禁止在平台上托管与加密货币相关的项目，理由涉及法律、伦理与安全等风险。这体现开源托管平台在开放协作与风险管理之间的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/33972/files" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI reduces Codex Model Context Size from 372k to 272k</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的上下文大小从 372k 减少到 272k</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts. Developers may need to restructure inputs or chunk code to preserve context, potentially affecting large-scale code generation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。开发者可能需要重新组织输入或将代码拆分成块以维持上下文，进而影响大规模代码生成的任务。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/07/19/top_stories_07192026'><span class='en'>Jul 19</span><span class='zh'>7月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>356</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965850' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>161</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-48972970" data-id="48972970" data-tags="Gaming|Open Source|Code" data-score="353" data-comments="147" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48972970" data-time="1784506610">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hgaiser/moonshine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Moonshine: Lets you stream games from your PC to any device running Moonlight</span><span class='zh'>Moonshine：让你把 PC 游戏流式传输到任何运行 Moonlight 的设备</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moonshine is a tool that enables streaming PC games to devices running Moonlight, expanding game streaming beyond official options.</span><span class='zh'>Moonshine 允许把电脑上的游戏流式传输到支持 Moonlight 的设备，拓展了官方 GameStream 的场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moonshine is a tool that enables streaming PC games to devices running Moonlight, expanding game streaming beyond official options. It highlights the ongoing ecosystem around Moonlight/GameStream, offering flexibility for multi-device play.</span><span class='zh'>Moonshine 允许把电脑上的游戏流式传输到支持 Moonlight 的设备，拓展了官方 GameStream 的场景。此工具彰显了 Moonlight/GameStream 生态的活跃性，为跨设备低延迟游戏体验提供新的可能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/stupside/castor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Better and Cheaper Than IPTV</span><span class='zh'>比 IPTV 更好更便宜的流媒体方案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Castor proposes a decentralized, open-source approach to streaming that aims to outperform traditional IPTV on cost and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>Castor 以去中心化、开源的流媒体方案为目标，力图在成本和鲁棒性上超过传统 IPTV。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Castor proposes a decentralized, open-source approach to streaming that aims to outperform traditional IPTV on cost and resilience. The project showcases how peer-to-peer tech and lightweight clients could reshape how video is delivered at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Castor 以去中心化、开源的流媒体方案为目标，力图在成本和鲁棒性上超过传统 IPTV。该项目展示了点对点技术与轻量客户端如何在大规模视频传输中重新定义交付方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/incremental" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Incremental – A library for incremental computations</span><span class='zh'>Incremental：一个用于增量计算的库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jane Street&#x27;s Incremental is a library that models dependencies and recomputations so only affected parts of a computation are updated, enabling efficient incremental workflows and reactive programming.</span><span class='zh'>Incremental 是 Jane Street 推出的增量计算库，通过跟踪依赖关系实现只对受影响的部分重新计算，从而提升性能与响应性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jane Street&#x27;s Incremental is a library that models dependencies and recomputations so only affected parts of a computation are updated, enabling efficient incremental workflows and reactive programming. It underpins complex dataflow systems and UI tooling by avoiding full recomputation.</span><span class='zh'>Incremental 是 Jane Street 推出的增量计算库，通过跟踪依赖关系实现只对受影响的部分重新计算，从而提升性能与响应性。它非常适合构建复杂数据流和响应式应用的增量工作流。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new agent skill enables forcing generated docs into ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English, standardizing wording for safety-critical or multinational audiences.</span><span class='zh'>新提出的代理技能使输出文档可强制采用 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语，帮助跨国与高要求场景的沟通一致性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new agent skill enables forcing generated docs into ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English, standardizing wording for safety-critical or multinational audiences. This approach could help reduce ambiguity in technical docs produced by AI assistants, easing localization and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>新提出的代理技能使输出文档可强制采用 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语，帮助跨国与高要求场景的沟通一致性。该方法有助于减少 AI 助手撰写技术文档时的歧义，便于本地化与合规。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/74066" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Potential session/cache leakage between workspace instances or consumer accounts</span><span class='zh'>工作区实例或消费账户间的会话/缓存泄漏潜在风险</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bug report highlights potential session or cache leakage across Claude Code workspace instances or consumer accounts, threatening data isolation in multi-tenant deployments.</span><span class='zh'>关于 Claude Code 的工作区实例之间或消费账户之间可能出现会话/缓存泄漏的问题报道，暴露多租户环境的数据隔离风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bug report highlights potential session or cache leakage across Claude Code workspace instances or consumer accounts, threatening data isolation in multi-tenant deployments. If real, it could let one user access another&#x27;s session data or cached prompts. Mitigations focus on stricter tenant isolation, scoped caching, shorter TTLs, and robust audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>关于 Claude Code 的工作区实例之间或消费账户之间可能出现会话/缓存泄漏的问题报道，暴露多租户环境的数据隔离风险。若属实，可能让一个用户访问到另一用户的会话数据或缓存提示。应对措施包括更严格的租户隔离、缓存作用域控制、缩短生存期以及完善的审计日志。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/07/04/top_stories_07042026'><span class='en'>Jul 4</span><span class='zh'>7月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>311</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48785485' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>129</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-48776464" data-id="48776464" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="303" data-comments="98" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776464" data-time="1783093849">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/teamchong/pxpipe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">412</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/teamchong/pxpipe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>60% Fable cost cut by converting code to images and having the model OCR it</span><span class='zh'>通过将代码转换为图像并让模型进行 OCR，Fable 成本降低 60%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cost-cutting experiment shows that encoding code as images and using OCR can dramatically reduce token usage in Fable, cutting costs by about 60%.</span><span class='zh'>在 Fable 的成本优化尝试中，將代码转成图像再用模型 OCR，大约降低 60% 的成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cost-cutting experiment shows that encoding code as images and using OCR can dramatically reduce token usage in Fable, cutting costs by about 60%. The approach highlights an unusual, input-format hack to optimize model usage, with trade-offs in accuracy and workflow.</span><span class='zh'>在 Fable 的成本优化尝试中，將代码转成图像再用模型 OCR，大约降低 60% 的成本。这一做法揭示了通过输入形式的创新来降低模型成本的潜在可能性，同时也伴随准确性和工作流的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google/copybara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google copybara: moving code between repositories</span><span class='zh'>Google Copybara：跨代码库迁移代码的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation. It helps teams maintain consistent workflows when moving assets, enabling cleaner separation of projects and easier collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。它帮助团队在跨仓库迁移时保持工作流的一致性，使项目分离更清晰、协作更高效。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vercel-labs/scriptc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scriptc by Vercel: TypeScript-to-Native compiler, no JavaScript engine in binary</span><span class='zh'>Vercel 的 Scriptc：TypeScript 转原生编译器，二进制中不含 JavaScript 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime. It promises faster startup and leaner binaries, but raises questions about ecosystem compatibility and platform coverage.</span><span class='zh'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。实现上可能带来启动更快、体积更小的优点，同时也带来生态兼容性与跨平台覆盖的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/searxng/searxng" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SearXNG: A free internet metasearch engine</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG：一个免费的互联网元搜索引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SearXNG is a free, open source metasearch engine that aggregates results from multiple sources while preserving user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG 是一个免费且开源的元搜索引擎，汇聚多家源端结果并强调用户隐私。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SearXNG is a free, open source metasearch engine that aggregates results from multiple sources while preserving user privacy. It is a modern fork with improved privacy defaults and straightforward self hosting, appealing to privacy minded researchers and admins.</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG 是一个免费且开源的元搜索引擎，汇聚多家源端结果并强调用户隐私。作为现代分支，它提供更严格的隐私默认和便于自托管的架构，适合注重隐私的研究者与系统管理员。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/31120" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JEP 401: Value Objects (Preview) merged to OpenJDK master</span><span class='zh'>JEP 401：值对象（预览版）已合并到 OpenJDK 主分支</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Value Objects, a Java feature designed to improve immutability and performance by avoiding identity semantics for small data, has been merged into OpenJDK master in preview form.</span><span class='zh'>值对象作为提升不可变性与性能的 Java 新特性，已以预览形式合并到 OpenJDK 主分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Value Objects, a Java feature designed to improve immutability and performance by avoiding identity semantics for small data, has been merged into OpenJDK master in preview form. The change could affect API design and hot-path optimization in Java apps. The preview invites early community feedback.</span><span class='zh'>值对象作为提升不可变性与性能的 Java 新特性，已以预览形式合并到 OpenJDK 主分支。若正式采纳，可能影响 Java 应用的 API 设计与热点路径优化。当前处于预览阶段，期待社区早期反馈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BadChemical/IoT-Vulnerability-Research-Public/blob/main/TP-Link_Kasa_EC71/Kasa_EC71.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TP-Link Kasa cameras leaked home GPS via unauthenticated UDP for 6 years</span><span class='zh'>TP-Link Kasa 摄像头六年来通过未认证的 UDP 泄露家庭位置信息</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 cameras were exposed to a long‑standing vulnerability where unauthenticated UDP traffic could leak sensitive GPS data.</span><span class='zh'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 摄像头长期存在未认证的 UDP 漏洞，可能泄露家庭位置信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 cameras were exposed to a long‑standing vulnerability where unauthenticated UDP traffic could leak sensitive GPS data. The issue underscores risks in legacy IoT designs and the need for firmware updates and network isolation.</span><span class='zh'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 摄像头长期存在未认证的 UDP 漏洞，可能泄露家庭位置信息。此事凸显了旧式物联网设计的风险，以及对固件更新和网络隔离的迫切需要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/juggler-ai/juggler" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Juggler – an open-source GUI coding agent, by the creator of JUCE</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Juggler——开源 GUI 编码代理，由 JUCE 创作者开发</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Juggler is an open-source GUI coding agent built by the JUCE creator, aimed at generating UI code and components automatically.</span><span class='zh'>Juggler 是由 JUCE 创作者开发的开源 GUI 编码代理，旨在自动生成界面代码与组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Juggler is an open-source GUI coding agent built by the JUCE creator, aimed at generating UI code and components automatically. It signals progress toward autonomous GUI tooling that can accelerate interface development.</span><span class='zh'>Juggler 是由 JUCE 创作者开发的开源 GUI 编码代理，旨在自动生成界面代码与组件。它标志着向自动化 GUI 工具迈进的进展，可能加速界面开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mokshablr/gander" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gander, an Android file viewer that asks for no permissions</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Gander——一个不请求权限的 Android 文件查看器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gander is an Android file viewer that operates without requesting runtime permissions, focusing on privacy and a minimal attack surface.</span><span class='zh'>Gander 是一款不请求运行时权限的 Android 文件查看器，聚焦隐私保护与低攻击面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gander is an Android file viewer that operates without requesting runtime permissions, focusing on privacy and a minimal attack surface. It uses system file pickers and scoped storage to access files securely, offering a lightweight alternative to typical file managers.</span><span class='zh'>Gander 是一款不请求运行时权限的 Android 文件查看器，聚焦隐私保护与低攻击面。它通过系统文件选择器与分区存储安全地访问文件，提供比常规文件管理器更轻量的替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chrislgarry/Apollo-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for command and lunar modules</span><span class='zh'>阿波罗11号导航计算机原始源代码（指挥舱与登陆舱）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The full original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for the Command and Lunar Modules has been published, offering a rare window into the software that steered humanity to the Moon.</span><span class='zh'>阿波罗11号导航计算机指挥舱与登陆舱的原始源代码已公开，为人们提供了了解引导人类登月的软件的珍贵机会。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The full original Apollo 11 Guidance Computer source code for the Command and Lunar Modules has been published, offering a rare window into the software that steered humanity to the Moon. The code highlights the era’s tight constraints, interrupt-driven real-time design, and enduring lessons for modern embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>阿波罗11号导航计算机指挥舱与登陆舱的原始源代码已公开，为人们提供了了解引导人类登月的软件的珍贵机会。代码展现了那个时代对资源极限的紧逼、事件驱动的实时设计，以及对现代嵌入式系统的持续启发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AARomanov1985/Audio-Cassette-Simulation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg</span><span class='zh'>用 FFmpeg 模拟磁带音频特性</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A GitHub project demonstrates how to simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg filters, capturing characteristics like frequency response and hiss.</span><span class='zh'>一个 GitHub 项目展示如何用 FFmpeg 过滤器模拟磁带音频特性，包含频响、嘶声等模拟参数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A GitHub project demonstrates how to simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg filters, capturing characteristics like frequency response and hiss. This enables retro-audio enthusiasts and preservation work to reproduce authentic analog textures in digital workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个 GitHub 项目展示如何用 FFmpeg 过滤器模拟磁带音频特性，包含频响、嘶声等模拟参数。对于复古音频爱好者和档案保护工作，这为在数字工作流中再现真实的磁带质感提供了工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB is a general-use object-oriented graph database</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB：通用型面向对象图数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB positions itself as a general-use object-oriented graph database, blending OO data modeling with graph traversal capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB 将自己定位为通用型的面向对象图数据库，将面向对象的数据建模与图遍历能力结合，提供一个统一的节点/边操作 API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB positions itself as a general-use object-oriented graph database, blending OO data modeling with graph traversal capabilities. It aims to offer a unified API for nodes and edges, appealing to developers who want both OOP ergonomics and rich graph queries.</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB 将自己定位为通用型的面向对象图数据库，将面向对象的数据建模与图遍历能力结合，提供一个统一的节点/边操作 API。对同时追求 OOP 体验与丰富图查询的开发者而言，这类数据库能简化数据建模与关系表达。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/80590" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Golang proposal: container/: generic collection types</span><span class='zh'>Go 提案：container/ 包中的泛型集合类型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go proposal to introduce generic collection types in the container/: package aims to provide type-safe, reusable data structures while preserving Go&#x27;s simplicity.</span><span class='zh'>Go 社区提出在 container/ 包中引入泛型集合类型的提案，目标是在保持简洁的前提下提供类型安全、可重用的数据结构。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new approach demonstrates how Claude can process and understand video content, bringing video understanding to large language models.</span><span class='zh'>一个方法让 Claude 能观看并理解视频内容，将大模型真正带入多模态感知。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/welcome-to-the-sunny-side/misa77" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: misa77 - a codec that decodes 2x faster than LZ4 (at better ratios)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：misa77——一个解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍、压缩比更佳的编解码器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>misa77 is a codec that lands faster decoding than LZ4 with better ratios.</span><span class='zh'>misa77 是一个编解码器，解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍且压缩效果更好。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>misa77 is a codec that lands faster decoding than LZ4 with better ratios. It challenges assumptions about speed vs. compression trade-offs and could influence storage and network efficiency in real-world apps. The release provides insights into practical codec design.</span><span class='zh'>misa77 是一个编解码器，解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍且压缩效果更好。这对存储与网络传输效率提出了新的可能，也展示了编码器设计中的权衡与取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cosmtrek/mindwalk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mindwalk – Replay coding-agent sessions on a 3D map of your codebase</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Mindwalk——在代码库的3D地图上回放编码代理会话</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mindwalk is a Show HN project that replays coding-agent sessions on a 3D map of your codebase.</span><span class='zh'>Mindwalk 是一个 Show HN 项目，能够在代码库的三维地图上回放编码代理的会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mindwalk is a Show HN project that replays coding-agent sessions on a 3D map of your codebase. It visually traces how a bot or AI assistant navigated and changed code, providing a retrospective view for debugging, learning, and collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Mindwalk 是一个 Show HN 项目，能够在代码库的三维地图上回放编码代理的会话。它直观地呈现代理在代码中的导航和修改轨迹，为调试、学习和协作提供回放视图。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Biff.graph lets you model a Clojure codebase as a graph that can be queried, enabling structural analysis and refactoring insights.</span><span class='zh'>Biff.graph 允许把 Clojure 代码库建模为一个可查询的图谱，便于进行结构分析和重构洞察。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A prototype shows Python 3.14 compiled to native code with no interpreter, delivering faster startup and efficiency gains.</span><span class='zh'>原型演示将 Python 3.14 编译为原生代码且不依赖解释器，提升启动速度与效率。</span></p>

It demonstrates ahead-of-time compilation trends and raises questions about compatibility and ecosystem support.</span><span class='zh'>原型演示将 Python 3.14 编译为原生代码且不依赖解释器，提升启动速度与效率。展现 Ahead-of-Time 编译的趋势，同时引发关于兼容性与生态系统支持的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Saivineeth147/lora-speedrun" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LoRA Speedrun – a public wall-clock leaderboard for fine-tuning techniques</span><span class='zh'>LoRA 速度跑：用于微调技术的公开墙钟式排行榜</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LoRA Speedrun publicly catalogs wall-clock times for LoRA-based fine-tuning runs, enabling apples-to-apples comparisons of efficiency across models and setups.</span><span class='zh'>LoRA Speedrun 将基于 LoRA 的微调过程按墙钟时间公开排序，便于横向比较不同模型与配置的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LoRA Speedrun publicly catalogs wall-clock times for LoRA-based fine-tuning runs, enabling apples-to-apples comparisons of efficiency across models and setups. By surfacing how implementation details and hardware affect speed, it nudges researchers toward faster, more reproducible fine-tuning practices.</span><span class='zh'>LoRA Speedrun 将基于 LoRA 的微调过程按墙钟时间公开排序，便于横向比较不同模型与配置的效率。该排行榜揭示实现细节和硬件选择对训练速度的影响，推动研究者追求更快、可复现的微调方法。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A case study details how a frontier lab&#x27;s agent was compromised, outlining attack vectors, security gaps in model deployment pipelines, and lessons for defending research-scale AI deployments. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/flythenimbus/bramble" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Bramble – Local-first password manager</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Bramble——本地优先的密码管理器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bramble is a local first password manager that prioritizes offline storage and privacy, with optional sync that preserves user control.</span><span class='zh'>Bramble 作为本地优先的密码管理器，强调离线存储与隐私保护，支持可控的同步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bramble is a local first password manager that prioritizes offline storage and privacy, with optional sync that preserves user control. The Show HN signals a compelling alternative to cloud centric password managers and invites discussion on threat models.</span><span class='zh'>Bramble 作为本地优先的密码管理器，强调离线存储与隐私保护，支持可控的同步。Show HN 的展示凸显了对比云端为主的密码管理方案的潜在优势，并引发关于威胁模型的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tokio-rs/topcoat" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Topcoat: The full full-stack framework for Rust</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat：Rust 的全栈框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Topcoat is a full-stack web framework built for Rust, aiming to streamline front-to-back web app development with asynchronous support and integrated tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat 是为 Rust 构建的全栈 Web 框架，旨在提供从前端到后端的一体化开发体系，具备异步支持和集成工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Topcoat is a full-stack web framework built for Rust, aiming to streamline front-to-back web app development with asynchronous support and integrated tooling. It competes with established Rust web ecosystems by offering cohesive routing, SSR, and API layers in one package.</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat 是为 Rust 构建的全栈 Web 框架，旨在提供从前端到后端的一体化开发体系，具备异步支持和集成工具。它通过统一的路由、服务端渲染和 API 层，试图与现有 Rust Web 生态系统竞争。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Poseidon-fan/linux-0.11-rs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux 0.11 rewritten in idiomatic Rust, boots in QEMU</span><span class='zh'>Linux 0.11 用地道 Rust 重写并在 QEMU 启动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An enthusiast rebuilt the Linux 0.11 kernel in idiomatic Rust and got it to boot in QEMU.</span><span class='zh'>有人用地道的 Rust 重写 Linux 0.11，并在 QEMU 中成功启动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An enthusiast rebuilt the Linux 0.11 kernel in idiomatic Rust and got it to boot in QEMU. The project demonstrates Rust’s viability for low-level systems work and serves as an educational bridge from classic kernels to modern tooling. It spotlights both the enduring complexity of OS internals and the expressive power of Rust abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>有人用地道的 Rust 重写 Linux 0.11，并在 QEMU 中成功启动。该项目展示了 Rust 在底层系统编程中的可行性，并为将经典内核迁移到现代工具链提供了学习路径。它揭示了操作系统内部的复杂性，以及 Rust 抽象能力的强大。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onecli/onecli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OneCLI – OSS credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI agents</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI：开源凭证网关，让秘密不落入 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OneCLI provides an open-source credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI prompts while still enabling automated workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 提供一个开源凭证网关，让秘密不再通过 AI 提示被暴露，同时仍然支持自动化工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OneCLI provides an open-source credential gateway that keeps secrets out of AI prompts while still enabling automated workflows. It aims to reduce risk from leaking credentials through model access.</span><span class='zh'>OneCLI 提供一个开源凭证网关，让秘密不再通过 AI 提示被暴露，同时仍然支持自动化工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JuliaLang/julia/pull/62334" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UnifiedIR for Julia</span><span class='zh'>Julia 的 UnifiedIR（统一中间表示）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features. If merged, this could simplify compiler maintenance and speed up experimentation with new targets.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。若合并，将简化编译器维护并加速对新目标的尝试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/karola3vax/CS2FOW" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War: Server-sided anti-wallhack occlusion culling for CS2 servers</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War：服务器端的遮挡剔除以对抗穿墙作弊</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War implements server-side occlusion culling to hide unseen geometry, reducing information leakage and helping prevent wallhack-style cheating.</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War 在服务器端实现遮挡剔除，隐藏玩家看不到的几何信息，降低穿墙作弊的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CS2 Fog Of War implements server-side occlusion culling to hide unseen geometry, reducing information leakage and helping prevent wallhack-style cheating. The approach shifts some visibility logic to the server, potentially impacting latency and fairness.</span><span class='zh'>CS2 Fog Of War 在服务器端实现遮挡剔除，隐藏玩家看不到的几何信息，降低穿墙作弊的可能性。该做法将可见性的一部分放在服务器端，可能对延迟与游戏公平性产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jbwinters/jacquard-lang" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Jacquard, a programming language for AI-written, human-reviewed code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Jacquard，一种用于 AI 生成、人工审核代码的编程语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jacquard proposes a programming language designed for AI-generated code that is nonetheless reviewed by humans.</span><span class='zh'>Jacquard 设计了一种用于 AI 生成、由人类审核代码的编程语言，强调可追溯性与可审查性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jacquard proposes a programming language designed for AI-generated code that is nonetheless reviewed by humans. It explores tooling for traceability, reproducibility, and safe AI-assisted software development.</span><span class='zh'>Jacquard 设计了一种用于 AI 生成、由人类审核代码的编程语言，强调可追溯性与可审查性。它探索了在 AI 辅助编程中实现更安全、可控的软件开发工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/houslast3/85.30-GFLOPS-Single-Core-FP32-Matrix-Multiplication-on-AMD-Zen-3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>85.3 GFlops: Optimizing FP32 Matrix Multiplication on a Single AMD Zen 3 Core</span><span class='zh'>单核 AMD Zen 3 的 FP32 矩阵乘法优化到 85.3 GFLOPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A benchmark-style writeup shows achieving 85.3 GFLOPS for FP32 matrix multiply on a single AMD Zen 3 core.</span><span class='zh'>一篇基准式的文章展示在单个 AMD Zen 3 内核上实现 FP32 矩阵乘法达到 85.3 GFLOPS 的结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A benchmark-style writeup shows achieving 85.3 GFLOPS for FP32 matrix multiply on a single AMD Zen 3 core. It covers techniques like vectorization, cache optimization, and instruction-level tuning, highlighting how much CPU microarchitectures can still surprise. Useful for performance engineers and hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>一篇基准式的文章展示在单个 AMD Zen 3 内核上实现 FP32 矩阵乘法达到 85.3 GFLOPS 的结果。文中讲解向量化、缓存优化和指令级调优等技巧，凸显 CPU 微架构仍有惊喜，适合性能工程师和爱好者参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dicroce/wyrm_math" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Wyrm – Solve algebra by touch, built on an open-source soundness engine</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Wyrm — 通过触觉解代数，基于开源 soundness 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wyrm lets users solve algebraic problems through touch-based interaction, built on an open-source soundness engine.</span><span class='zh'>Wyrm 让用户通过触觉交互来求解代数题，底层依赖开源的 soundness 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wyrm lets users solve algebraic problems through touch-based interaction, built on an open-source soundness engine. The project demonstrates novel human-computer interaction for math education and accessibility, potentially enabling new ways to teach and explore algebraic concepts. It also provides a glimpse into how formal-method tooling can empower creative coding.</span><span class='zh'>Wyrm 让用户通过触觉交互来求解代数题，底层依赖开源的 soundness 引擎。该项目展示了用于数学教育和无障碍的新型人机交互方式，或为教学和探索代数概念带来新路径，同时体现了形式化工具在创意编码中的潜在应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/514-labs/dnsglobe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DNSGlobe – Rust TUI to watch DNS propagate around the world</span><span class='zh'>DNSGlobe：用 Rust 构建的文本界面监视全球 DNS 传播</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DNSGlobe is a Rust-based TUI that visualizes DNS propagation around the world, offering a lightweight tool for operators to observe latency, regional differences, and caching behavior.</span><span class='zh'>DNSGlobe 是基于 Rust 的 TUI 工具，用以可视化全球范围内的 DNS 传播情况，方便运维观察延迟和区域差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DNSGlobe is a Rust-based TUI that visualizes DNS propagation around the world, offering a lightweight tool for operators to observe latency, regional differences, and caching behavior. It demonstrates how terminal apps can serve practical network insight without heavy dashboards.</span><span class='zh'>DNSGlobe 是基于 Rust 的 TUI 工具，用以可视化全球范围内的 DNS 传播情况，方便运维观察延迟和区域差异。展示了在运维场景中，文本界面也能提供实用的网络洞察。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/CharmingBlaze/moonbasic" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MoonBASIC: A modern BASIC for building 2D and 3D games</span><span class='zh'>MoonBASIC：用于构建 2D/3D 游戏的现代 BASIC</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoonBASIC is a modern take on BASIC designed for building 2D and 3D games, blending approachable syntax with contemporary tooling.</span><span class='zh'>MoonBASIC 是对 BASIC 的现代化演绎，面向 2D/3D 游戏开发，结合易用语法与现代工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoonBASIC is a modern take on BASIC designed for building 2D and 3D games, blending approachable syntax with contemporary tooling. It illustrates how retro-inspired languages can evolve for modern game development and education.</span><span class='zh'>MoonBASIC 是对 BASIC 的现代化演绎，面向 2D/3D 游戏开发，结合易用语法与现代工具链。它展示了复古风格的语言如何进化以用于现代游戏开发与教育。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jdefrancesco/dskDitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DskDitto: Ultra-fast, parallel duplicate-file detector</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto：极快的并行重复文件检测器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DskDitto delivers ultra-fast, parallel duplicate detection by exploiting multi-core CPUs, enabling scalable dedup across large datasets.</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto 通过多核并行实现超快的重复文件检测，能够对海量数据集进行高效去重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DskDitto delivers ultra-fast, parallel duplicate detection by exploiting multi-core CPUs, enabling scalable dedup across large datasets. It helps reclaim disk space, streamline backups, and can fit into build or CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>DskDitto 通过多核并行实现超快的重复文件检测，能够对海量数据集进行高效去重。帮助释放磁盘空间、简化备份，并可无缝集成到构建或 CI 流水线中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ctxrs/ctx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: ctx – Search the coding agent history already on your machine</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：ctx——在本地就能搜索你的编码代理历史</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ctx is a local-first tool that indexes and lets you search coding-agent history on your computer, enabling you to recall past interactions without cloud lookups.</span><span class='zh'>ctx 是一个本地优先的工具，可对你机器上的编码代理历史进行索引与检索，让你在本地回溯过去的交互记录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ctx is a local-first tool that indexes and lets you search coding-agent history on your computer, enabling you to recall past interactions without cloud lookups.</span><span class='zh'>ctx 是一个本地优先的工具，可对你机器上的编码代理历史进行索引与检索，让你在本地回溯过去的交互记录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Rodiun/frugon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Frugon – Find which LLM calls a cheaper model could handle (local, MIT)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Frugon—找出哪些 LLM 调用可以由更便宜的本地模型处理（MIT 开源）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frugon is a local-first tool that analyzes prompts to determine whether a cheaper model could handle the LLM call, helping teams cut inference costs without sacrificing quality.</span><span class='zh'>Frugon 是一款本地优先的工具，能够分析提示并判断是否可由更便宜的模型处理某些 LLM 调用，从而降低推理成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frugon is a local-first tool that analyzes prompts to determine whether a cheaper model could handle the LLM call, helping teams cut inference costs without sacrificing quality. Built with MIT-licensed tooling, it helps teams optimize inference spend in real-world workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Frugon 是一款本地优先的工具，能够分析提示并判断是否可由更便宜的模型处理某些 LLM 调用，从而降低推理成本。该工具使用本地托管、MIT 许可的组件，帮助团队在实际工作负载中优化成本。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates optimizing and serving models with quality comparable to established baselines at about half the cost, signaling more accessible model hosting for smaller teams and experiments.</span><span class='zh'>该项目展示在质量接近 Fable 等知名基线的前提下，以约一半成本进行模型优化与部署，意味着较小团队也能更经济地托管模型进行试验。</span></p>
