<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pIat0n/BareMetal-RAM-Dumper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BareMetal RAM Dumper – Bare-metal x86 tool for Cold Boot Attack experiments</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper：用于冷启动攻击实验的 Bare-metal x86 工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bare-metal RAM dumper enables memory dumps during cold-boot attack demonstrations, useful for hardware security researchers exploring memory residency and data remnants.</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper 是一款在裸机上执行内存转储的工具，便于在冷启动攻击演示中提取内存数据，帮助研究者研究内存留存与敏感信息泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bare-metal RAM dumper enables memory dumps during cold-boot attack demonstrations, useful for hardware security researchers exploring memory residency and data remnants. The tool lowers the barrier to reproduce classic attack scenarios on x86 hardware, but underscores ethics and legal boundaries in performing such experiments.</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper 是一款在裸机上执行内存转储的工具，便于在冷启动攻击演示中提取内存数据，帮助研究者研究内存留存与敏感信息泄露。该工具降低了在 x86 硬件上复现传统攻击情景的门槛，但同时必须遵循伦理和法律边界。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/07/04/top_stories_07042026'><span class='en'>Jul 4</span><span class='zh'>7月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>62</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787201' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49126883" data-id="49126883" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="61" data-comments="39" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126883" data-time="1785522488">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/l00sed/termixer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Termixer (TUI DJ Mixer)</span><span class='zh'>Termixer（基于文本界面的 DJ 混音器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Termixer is a terminal-based DJ mixer UI built with a TUI framework, enabling live mixing in a console environment.</span><span class='zh'>Termixer 是一个在终端运行的 DJ 混音器界面，演示了如何在无 GUI 的环境中进行现场混音。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Termixer is a terminal-based DJ mixer UI built with a TUI framework, enabling live mixing in a console environment. It demonstrates how creative CLI tools can replace GUI workflows and offer lightweight, scriptable audio control.</span><span class='zh'>Termixer 是一个在终端运行的 DJ 混音器界面，演示了如何在无 GUI 的环境中进行现场混音。它凸显了 CLI 工具在音频工作流中的创新用途，以及轻量化、可脚本化的控制方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lopopolo/harness-engineering" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Harness Engineering</span><span class='zh'>Harness 工程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Harness Engineering presents a framework or collection of practices for building and using harnesses to test, benchmark, or orchestrate experiments in software and ML systems.</span><span class='zh'>Harness Engineering 提供构建与使用“工具箱/对接”以测试、基准测试或编排软件与机器学习系统实验的实践框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Harness Engineering presents a framework or collection of practices for building and using harnesses to test, benchmark, or orchestrate experiments in software and ML systems.</span><span class='zh'>Harness Engineering 提供构建与使用“工具箱/对接”以测试、基准测试或编排软件与机器学习系统实验的实践框架。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zig now supports packaging and cross-compiling C/C++ projects, easing interop and potentially improving build reliability and cross-platform binaries.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 现在支持打包和跨平台编译 C/C++ 项目，简化互操作性，提升构建可靠性与跨平台二进制的可预测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zig now supports packaging and cross-compiling C/C++ projects, easing interop and potentially improving build reliability and cross-platform binaries. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/n67094/sdl_gp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU minimal, single-header, high-performance 2D graphics painting library</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU：极简单头文件的高性能二维绘画库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU is a minimal, single-header library for high-performance 2D graphics painting with no dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU 是一个极简且单头文件的高性能二维绘图库，无需外部依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SDL_GPU is a minimal, single-header library for high-performance 2D graphics painting with no dependencies. It aims to be easy to integrate into games and tools that need fast rendering without heavyweight graphics stacks. It&#x27;s a practical option when you want portability and speed with minimal setup.</span><span class='zh'>SDL_GPU 是一个极简且单头文件的高性能二维绘图库，无需外部依赖。它面向需要快速渲染的游戏和工具，减少了图形栈的复杂性，便于快速集成并保持跨平台性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tucan9389/nanochat-jax/discussions/1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Porting nanochat to a TPU: what carries over from PyTorch, and what breaks</span><span class='zh'>将 NanoChat 移植到 TPU：哪些来自 PyTorch 的特性可用，哪些会出错</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Porting a neural chat model to TPUs reveals where PyTorch workflows map cleanly to accelerators and where custom code breaks due to XLA constraints.</span><span class='zh'>将一个神经聊天模型移植到 TPU 的过程揭示了哪些 PyTorch 的工作流能平滑映射到加速器、哪些在 XLA 的约束下会出错。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Porting a neural chat model to TPUs reveals where PyTorch workflows map cleanly to accelerators and where custom code breaks due to XLA constraints. The effort highlights the friction between dynamic PyTorch models and the static, compiled nature of TPU backends.</span><span class='zh'>将一个神经聊天模型移植到 TPU 的过程揭示了哪些 PyTorch 的工作流能平滑映射到加速器、哪些在 XLA 的约束下会出错。该工作凸显了动态的 PyTorch 模型与 TPU 后端的静态编译特性之间的摩擦。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/07/18/top_stories_07182026'><span class='en'>Jul 18</span><span class='zh'>7月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>50</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917755' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>9</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-48800930" data-id="48800930" data-tags="Science|Code" data-score="50" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48800930" data-time="1783315582">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/aatishb/patterncollider" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1704</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aatishb/patterncollider" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Patterncollider: Generate and explore quasiperiodic tiling patterns</span><span class='zh'>Patterncollider：生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Patterncollider, a tool to generate and explore quasiperiodic tilings, enabling interactive study of non-repeating patterns like Penrose tilings.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Patterncollider，这一工具用于生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案，支持对非重复图案（如 Penrose 镶嵌）进行交互式研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Patterncollider, a tool to generate and explore quasiperiodic tilings, enabling interactive study of non-repeating patterns like Penrose tilings. It invites mathematical exploration and potential applications in art and game design.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Patterncollider，这一工具用于生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案，支持对非重复图案（如 Penrose 镶嵌）进行交互式研究。适用于数学爱好者，以及在艺术和游戏设计中的潜在应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vercel-labs/deepsec" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Deepsec</span><span class='zh'>DeepSec 安全大会</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSec is a security conference; the repository collects talks, materials, and potentially talks on threat intelligence, security research, and defense techniques.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSec 是一场安全大会，该仓库汇集了演讲、材料和研究成果，涵盖威胁情报、漏洞研究与防御技术等主题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSec is a security conference; the repository collects talks, materials, and potentially talks on threat intelligence, security research, and defense techniques. It serves as a hub for researchers to share findings and for practitioners to stay updated on emerging attack surfaces.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSec 是一场安全大会，该仓库汇集了演讲、材料和研究成果，涵盖威胁情报、漏洞研究与防御技术等主题。它为研究者分享发现、从业者了解新兴攻击面提供了一个集中的资源入口。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Cpp2Rust/cpp2rust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust: Translates C++ to safe Rust automatically</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust：自动将 C++ 转换为安全 Rust</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust automatically translates C++ code to Rust with safety guarantees, easing migration of legacy codebases.</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust 能自动将 C++ 代码转换为具有安全性保证的 Rust，帮助遗留代码的迁移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cpp2Rust automatically translates C++ code to Rust with safety guarantees, easing migration of legacy codebases. While it promises safer memory management, developers should scrutinize correctness and performance post-migration and plan for integration challenges. It opens a path to safer reimplementations of old code.</span><span class='zh'>Cpp2Rust 能自动将 C++ 代码转换为具有安全性保证的 Rust，帮助遗留代码的迁移。虽然承诺更安全的内存管理，但迁移后仍需对正确性和性能进行审查，并考虑集成中的挑战。为旧代码提供了更安全的重实现路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/RayTracer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ray Tracer in SQL</span><span class='zh'>SQL 实现的光线追踪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ClickHouse&#x27;s Ray Tracer in SQL demonstrates how expressive SQL can be when used for graphics-like workloads, implementing ray tracing entirely in SQL primitives.</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse 将光线追踪完全用 SQL 原语实现，展示了 SQL 在图形化工作负载中的表达力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ClickHouse&#x27;s Ray Tracer in SQL demonstrates how expressive SQL can be when used for graphics-like workloads, implementing ray tracing entirely in SQL primitives. The project serves as a provocative proof of concept and a pointer to what’s possible with vectorized engines. It invites exploration of SQL-based rendering techniques and performance considerations.</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse 将光线追踪完全用 SQL 原语实现，展示了 SQL 在图形化工作负载中的表达力。该项目是一种挑衅性的概念证明，指向向量化引擎潜在的可能性，同时促使人们探讨基于 SQL 的渲染技术及其性能边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/schollz/croc/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Croc: Securely transfer files and folders between two computers</span><span class='zh'>Croc：在两台计算机之间安全传输文件和文件夹</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Croc is a simple CLI tool that securely transfers files between machines via encrypted channels, emphasizing ease of use and cross‑platform compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Croc 是一个简洁的命令行工具，利用加密通道在两台计算机之间安全传输文件，强调易用性与跨平台兼容性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/robertsdotpm/runloom" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Runloom – Go-style coroutines for Python free-threaded</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Runloom——为 Python 提供 Go 风格的协程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Runloom brings Go-style coroutines to Python, enabling more approachable concurrency without heavy threading.</span><span class='zh'>Runloom 将 Go 风格的协程带进 Python，使并发编程更加友好，减少对多线程的痛点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Runloom brings Go-style coroutines to Python, enabling more approachable concurrency without heavy threading. It could ease async programming for Python developers and spark new patterns for parallel tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Runloom 将 Go 风格的协程带进 Python，使并发编程更加友好，减少对多线程的痛点。对 Python 开发者的异步编程或将带来更易用的新范式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/newren/git-filter-repo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quickly rewrite Git repository history</span><span class='zh'>快速重写 Git 仓库历史</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git-filter-repo is a fast tool for rewriting Git history, enabling tasks such as removing sensitive data, changing authors, or pruning large files with safer defaults than older methods.</span><span class='zh'>git-filter-repo 提供快速、安全的历史重写能力，支持删除大文件、清除敏感数据、修正作者等操作，相较旧工具有更友善的默认选项。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Git-filter-repo is a fast tool for rewriting Git history, enabling tasks such as removing sensitive data, changing authors, or pruning large files with safer defaults than older methods. It includes practical examples and tips for patching history without breaking repos.</span><span class='zh'>git-filter-repo 提供快速、安全的历史重写能力，支持删除大文件、清除敏感数据、修正作者等操作，相较旧工具有更友善的默认选项。文中给出实用示例与使用要点，帮助在不破坏仓库的前提下改写历史。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xhluca/agent-talk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agent-talk: Enabling coding agents to work together</span><span class='zh'>Agent-talk：让编码智能体协同工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agent-talk is a framework enabling multiple coding agents to coordinate and collaborate on tasks, potentially improving automation efficiency and reducing deadlock through structured communication.</span><span class='zh'>Agent-talk 提供一个框架，使多个编码智能体能够协同完成任务，通过结构化通信提升自动化效率并减少协作冲突。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent-talk is a framework enabling multiple coding agents to coordinate and collaborate on tasks, potentially improving automation efficiency and reducing deadlock through structured communication.</span><span class='zh'>Agent-talk 提供一个框架，使多个编码智能体能够协同完成任务，通过结构化通信提升自动化效率并减少协作冲突。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ariel95500-create/gp-elite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pure-Python symbolic regression that rediscovered Kepler&#x27;s law from 8 data point</span><span class='zh'>纯 Python 符号回归用 8 个数据点重新发现 Kepler 定律</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pure-Python symbolic regression approach discovers Kepler&#x27;s laws from just eight data points, illustrating how symbolic AI can extract fundamental physics from small datasets.</span><span class='zh'>纯 Python 符号回归方法仅用 8 个数据点就重新发现了开普勒定律，展示了符号 AI 能从小样本中提取基本物理规律的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pure-Python symbolic regression approach discovers Kepler&#x27;s laws from just eight data points, illustrating how symbolic AI can extract fundamental physics from small datasets. This demonstrates the potential for AI-driven scientific discovery with accessible tooling.</span><span class='zh'>纯 Python 符号回归方法仅用 8 个数据点就重新发现了开普勒定律，展示了符号 AI 能从小样本中提取基本物理规律的潜力。这表明在可访问的工具下，AI 驱动的科学发现具有可实现性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PennyLaneAI/pennylane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PennyLane is an open-source quantum software platform for quantum</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane：开源量子软件平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PennyLane enables differentiable programming across quantum hardware and simulators, democratizing access to quantum machine learning and variational algorithms.</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane 提供可在量子硬件与模拟器之间进行可微分编程的框架，使量子机器学习和变分算法更易接触。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PennyLane enables differentiable programming across quantum hardware and simulators, democratizing access to quantum machine learning and variational algorithms. Its open-source nature and framework integrations lower barriers to experimenting with quantum ML.</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane 提供可在量子硬件与模拟器之间进行可微分编程的框架，使量子机器学习和变分算法更易接触。作为开源项目，良好的生态集成降低了入门与试验的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/github/freno" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Freno: cooperative, highly available throttler service</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Freno：协作、高度可用的限流服务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Freno is a cooperative, highly available throttling service used to enforce rate limits across distributed systems.</span><span class='zh'>Freno 是一个协作式、高可用的限流服务，用于跨分布式系统执行速率限制，展示了在大规模平台中实现可靠性与协作所有权的模式。</span></p>

It highlights patterns for reliability and shared ownership in large-scale platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Freno 是一个协作式、高可用的限流服务，用于跨分布式系统执行速率限制，展示了在大规模平台中实现可靠性与协作所有权的模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/1ay1/agentty" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agentty – A drop-in alternative to claude-code, written in C++26. 11.0 MB binary</span><span class='zh'>Agentty——一个替代 Claude-code 的就地集成实现，采用 C++26 编写，11.0 MB 二进制</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agentty is a compact code-completion assistant, designed as a drop-in alternative to Claude-code, implemented in C++26 with a tiny binary footprint.</span><span class='zh'>Agentty 是一个体积小巧、可替代 Claude-code 的代码助手，使用 C++26 实现，二进制仅约11MB。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agentty is a compact code-completion assistant, designed as a drop-in alternative to Claude-code, implemented in C++26 with a tiny binary footprint. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rejourneyco/rejourney" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rejourney – Open-source revenue leak prediction for web and mobile apps</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Rejourney 開源的網頁/移動應用營收流失預測工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rejourney is an open-source tool that predicts revenue leakage for web and mobile apps, helping teams pinpoint where money slips away in funnels.</span><span class='zh'>Rejourney 是一個開源工具，用於預測網頁與行動應用的營收流失，協助團隊找出漏失收入的環節。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rejourney is an open-source tool that predicts revenue leakage for web and mobile apps, helping teams pinpoint where money slips away in funnels. Its open-source nature invites community contributions and rapid iteration on monetization models.</span><span class='zh'>Rejourney 是一個開源工具，用於預測網頁與行動應用的營收流失，協助團隊找出漏失收入的環節。其開源特性促使社群貢獻與快速迭代在變現模型上的改進。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adam-s/car-diagnosis" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Classify mechanical faults using Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用对比语言-音频预训练来分类机械故障</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight demonstration shows how Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) can classify mechanical faults from audio signals using simple prompts.</span><span class='zh'>一个简短的示例展示利用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）从声音信号中分类机械故障，并通过简单提示实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight demonstration shows how Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) can classify mechanical faults from audio signals using simple prompts. It highlights a practical path toward data-efficient fault diagnosis that can reduce downtime and maintenance costs.</span><span class='zh'>一个简短的示例展示利用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）从声音信号中分类机械故障，并通过简单提示实现。该方案体现了数据高效的故障诊断潜力，有望降低停机时间与维护成本。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1982</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/cryptography-research-demo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic publishes a practical key-recovery attack on HAWK-256</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布对 HAWK-256 的实用密钥恢复攻击</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic released a demonstration showing a practical key-recovery attack against the HAWK-256 cryptosystem, highlighting non-trivial weaknesses in the scheme.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了对 HAWK-256 公钥或私钥的实用恢复攻击演示，暴露该密码体制的实际脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic released a demonstration showing a practical key-recovery attack against the HAWK-256 cryptosystem, highlighting non-trivial weaknesses in the scheme. The result is a reminder that cryptography must be carefully evaluated before deployment in security-critical contexts, especially in AI safety tooling where crypto is used for safeguards. The work likely includes a reproducible demo with steps and implications for protocol designers.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了对 HAWK-256 公钥或私钥的实用恢复攻击演示，暴露该密码体制的实际脆弱性。此结果提醒在安全场景中部署前需对密码学方案进行严格评估，尤其是在 AI 安全工具中对加密的依赖。相关工作包含可复现的演示与对系统设计者的潜在影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kastor offers Terraform-style specifications for composing AI agents, enabling declarative definitions of agent capabilities and workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Kastor 提供了面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格，使代理能力与工作流的声明性定义成为可能。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2014</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bkuan001/halo-record" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Halo – open-source, tamper-evident runtime evidence for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Halo：开源、防篡改的 AI 代理运行时证据</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Halo records tamper-evident runtime evidence of AI agent actions, enabling auditing and accountability after the fact.</span><span class='zh'>Halo 能以防篡改的方式记录 AI 代理行动的运行时证据，便于事后审计与问责，是提升 AI 系统可验证溯源性的一个重要步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Halo records tamper-evident runtime evidence of AI agent actions, enabling auditing and accountability after the fact. It represents a step toward verifiable provenance in AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Halo 能以防篡改的方式记录 AI 代理行动的运行时证据，便于事后审计与问责，是提升 AI 系统可验证溯源性的一个重要步骤。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2018</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gruns/icecream" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceCream – Never use print() to debug again</span><span class='zh'>IceCream：再也不用 print() 调试了</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceCream provides a lightweight debugging helper for Python that prints values with context using ic(), offering nicer output than plain print statements.</span><span class='zh'>IceCream 是一个 Python 调试工具，使用 ic() 打印变量及上下文信息，比普通 print 输出更易读。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceCream provides a lightweight debugging helper for Python that prints values with context using ic(), offering nicer output than plain print statements. It supports nested data structures and stack trace hints, making ad-hoc debugging more efficient.</span><span class='zh'>IceCream 是一个 Python 调试工具，使用 ic() 打印变量及上下文信息，比普通 print 输出更易读。它支持嵌套数据和栈信息，能提升随手调试的效率。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2038</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Qbix/webserver/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A PHP server that can handle 10x as many concurrent requests as Nginx+PHP-fpm</span><span class='zh'>一个能够承载比 Nginx+PHP-FPM 高出十倍并发请求的 PHP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A PHP server claims to handle an order of magnitude more concurrent requests than Nginx+PHP-FPM by employing an optimized event-driven architecture and smarter resource management.</span><span class='zh'>一个自研 PHP 服务器声称在并发请求数上比 Nginx+PHP-FPM 高出十倍，源于事件驱动架构与高效资源管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A PHP server claims to handle an order of magnitude more concurrent requests than Nginx+PHP-FPM by employing an optimized event-driven architecture and smarter resource management. The README details architecture choices and benchmark results.</span><span class='zh'>一个自研 PHP 服务器声称在并发请求数上比 Nginx+PHP-FPM 高出十倍，源于事件驱动架构与高效资源管理。自述中列出架构设计要点与基准测试结果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cwida/ALP" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ALP: Adaptive lossless floating-point compression</span><span class='zh'>ALP：自适应无损浮点压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ALP is a library for adaptive lossless floating-point compression that aims to reduce memory bandwidth and storage without sacrificing precision.</span><span class='zh'>ALP 是一个自适应无损浮点数据压缩库，目标是在不损失精度的前提下降低内存带宽与存储需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ALP is a library for adaptive lossless floating-point compression that aims to reduce memory bandwidth and storage without sacrificing precision.</span><span class='zh'>ALP 是一个自适应无损浮点数据压缩库，目标是在不损失精度的前提下降低内存带宽与存储需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates installing and running PostgreSQL without Docker, Homebrew, or apt, likely via pip-based tooling or portable artifacts.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何在不依赖 Docker、Homebrew 和 apt 的情况下安装并运行 PostgreSQL，可能借助 pip 工具链或可移植组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates installing and running PostgreSQL without Docker, Homebrew, or apt, likely via pip-based tooling or portable artifacts. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mulfyx/w4me-station" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>W4ME Station – a WASM-4 runtime for Java ME phones</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station：为 Java ME 手机实现的 WASM-4 运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>W4ME Station ships a WASM-4 runtime compatible with Java ME devices, letting modern WASM-based games run on feature phones.</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station 为 Java ME 手机提供了 WASM-4 运行时，使现代 WASM 游戏能够在功能手机上运行，展示了紧凑运行时如何让老设备焕发新生，并接入现代网页式体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>W4ME Station ships a WASM-4 runtime compatible with Java ME devices, letting modern WASM-based games run on feature phones. It demonstrates how compact runtimes can bridge old hardware with contemporary web-like experiences.</span><span class='zh'>W4ME Station 为 Java ME 手机提供了 WASM-4 运行时，使现代 WASM 游戏能够在功能手机上运行，展示了紧凑运行时如何让老设备焕发新生，并接入现代网页式体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A local merge queue coordinates parallel Claude Code agents to work on the same codebase, speeding experimentation and reducing bottlenecks in AI-assisted development.</span><span class='zh'>本地合并队列用于让多个 Claude Code 代理在同一代码库上并行工作，加速实验并减少 AI 辅助开发中的瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A local merge queue coordinates parallel Claude Code agents to work on the same codebase, speeding experimentation and reducing bottlenecks in AI-assisted development.
